## 第十四轮洞察：量子化认知状态——知识的叠加态与观测坍缩

### 第一性原理洞察

经典知识管理假设知识是确定态（0或1），但真实认知是叠加态（0和1同时存在，直到被观测）。如”Partner Matcher算法”在未被具体业务场景观测前，应同时保持”规则匹配”和”神经网络”两种可能态，根据查询上下文坍缩为特定实现。

### 方法论：Quantum-inspired Knowledge Superposition

建立量子知识比特（Qubit-Knowledge）： - 叠加态存储：一个知识晶体包含多种互斥实现（如SQL vs NoSQL方案） - 观测算符（Observable）：查询条件作为观测，触发波函数坍缩 - 纠缠（Entanglement）：关联知识间存在非局域关联，测量一个瞬间确定另一个

### 技术选型理由

为何用概率幅而非简单概率：概率幅可存在相位干涉（建设性/破坏性），模拟”不同知识路径的共振/抵消”。

为何本地模拟：真正的量子计算需QPU，但张量积状态空间可用NumPy模拟，单机实现量子认知语义。

### 代码级实施方案

# quantum_cognition/quantum_knowledge_space.py
# 量子化认知状态：叠加、纠缠、观测坍缩

import numpy as np
import sqlite3
import json
import hashlib
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Tuple, Set
from dataclasses import dataclass
from enum import Enum
import math

class BasisState(Enum):
    """认知基态（类似量子计算基矢|0⟩,|1⟩）"""
    IMPLEMENTATION_A = "impl_a"      # 如：规则引擎方案
    IMPLEMENTATION_B = "impl_b"      # 如：神经网络方案
    IMPLEMENTATION_C = "impl_c"      # 如：混合方案
    UNCERTAIN = "superposition"      # 叠加态

@dataclass
class KnowledgeQubit:
    """知识量子比特"""
    crystal_id: str
    amplitude: np.ndarray            # 概率幅向量 [a, b, c...]，复数
    basis_states: List[str]          # 基态标签（如['规则', '神经网络', '混合']）
    entangled_with: Set[str]         # 纠缠的知识晶体ID
    coherence_time: float            # 相干时间（多久未观测会退相干）
    last_observation: Optional[str]  # 上次观测结果（坍缩历史）

class QuantumKnowledgeSpace:
    """
    量子知识空间：管理知识的叠加态与纠缠关系
    物理意义：同一业务概念的多实现方案在未观测前共存
    """
    
    def __init__(self, db_path: str = "quantum_cognition/qks.db", dim: int = 3):
        self.conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path)
        self.dim = dim  # 默认3维（支持3种互斥实现）
        self._init_quantum_schema()
        self.hilbert_cache: Dict[str, KnowledgeQubit] = {}
        
        # 普朗克时间等效：观测精度（影响坍缩阈值）
        self.observation_threshold = 0.85
    
    def _init_quantum_schema(self):
        """量子知识Schema（存储密度矩阵）"""
        self.conn.executescript("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS quantum_states (
                crystal_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                amplitude_real TEXT,  -- JSON数组，复数实部
                amplitude_imag TEXT,  -- JSON数组，复数虚部
                basis_states TEXT,    -- JSON数组，基态标签
                entangled_with TEXT,  -- JSON数组，纠缠ID列表
                density_matrix TEXT,  -- JSON，密度矩阵ρ（用于退相干计算）
                coherence_ttl INTEGER, -- 相干时间（秒）
                last_observed TIMESTAMP
            );
            
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS observation_history (
                obs_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                crystal_id TEXT,
                query_context TEXT,
                collapsed_state TEXT,
                probability REAL,     -- 坍缩到该态的概率|α|²
                observation_time TIMESTAMP,
                observer_signature TEXT  -- 观测者（哪个模块触发）
            );
            
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS entanglement_registry (
                pair_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                crystal_a TEXT,
                crystal_b TEXT,
                entanglement_strength REAL,  -- 纠缠强度（贝尔不等式 violation量）
                created_at TIMESTAMP
            );
        """)
        self.conn.commit()
    
    def create_superposition(self, crystal_id: str, 
                            implementations: List[Dict],
                            weights: Optional[List[complex]] = None) -> KnowledgeQubit:
        """
        创建知识叠加态
        implementations: [{'name': '规则引擎', 'content': '...'}, ...]
        默认等权重叠加，若指定weights则自定义概率幅
        """
        n = len(implementations)
        self.dim = max(self.dim, n)
        
        if weights is None:
            # 等概率幅叠加（|ψ⟩ = (|a⟩+|b⟩+|c⟩)/√3）
            amplitude = np.ones(n, dtype=complex) / np.sqrt(n)
        else:
            # 归一化
            amplitude = np.array(weights, dtype=complex)
            norm = np.sqrt(np.sum(np.abs(amplitude)**2))
            amplitude = amplitude / norm
        
        # 密度矩阵 ρ = |ψ⟩⟨ψ|
        density_matrix = np.outer(amplitude, amplitude.conj())
        
        # 持久化
        self._persist_quantum_state(crystal_id, amplitude, 
                                   [impl['name'] for impl in implementations],
                                   density_matrix)
        
        qk = KnowledgeQubit(
            crystal_id=crystal_id,
            amplitude=amplitude,
            basis_states=[impl['name'] for impl in implementations],
            entangled_with=set(),
            coherence_time=3600,  # 默认1小时相干时间
            last_observation=None
        )
        
        self.hilbert_cache[crystal_id] = qk
        return qk
    
    def _persist_quantum_state(self, crystal_id: str, amp: np.ndarray, 
                              basis: List[str], rho: np.ndarray):
        """持久化量子态（密度矩阵形式防退相干）"""
        self.conn.execute("""
            INSERT OR REPLACE INTO quantum_states 
            (crystal_id, amplitude_real, amplitude_imag, basis_states,
             density_matrix, coherence_ttl, last_observed)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
        """, (
            crystal_id,
            json.dumps(amp.real.tolist()),
            json.dumps(amp.imag.tolist()),
            json.dumps(basis),
            json.dumps(rho.tolist()),
            3600,
            None
        ))
        self.conn.commit()
    
    def observe(self, crystal_id: str, query_context: str, 
                observable_operator: Optional[np.ndarray] = None) -> Dict:
        """
        观测算符：导致波函数坍缩
        query_context: 查询上下文（如"高频交易场景"）决定坍缩倾向
        """
        qk = self._load_qubit(crystal_id)
        if qk is None:
            return {'error': 'Quantum state not found'}
        
        # 构建观测算符（基于查询上下文的投影矩阵）
        if observable_operator is None:
            observable = self._build_observable_from_context(qk, query_context)
        else:
            observable = observable_operator
        
        # 计算期望值 ⟨ψ|Ô|ψ⟩
        expectation = np.vdot(qk.amplitude, np.dot(observable, qk.amplitude))
        
        # 坍缩概率分布（Born规则 |α_i|²）
        probabilities = np.abs(qk.amplitude)**2
        
        # 执行坍缩（蒙特卡洛采样）
        collapsed_idx = np.random.choice(len(qk.basis_states), p=probabilities.real)
        collapsed_state = qk.basis_states[collapsed_idx]
        
        # 记录观测历史
        self.conn.execute("""
            INSERT INTO observation_history 
            (crystal_id, query_context, collapsed_state, probability, observation_time, observer_signature)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, datetime('now'), 'quantum_observer')
        """, (crystal_id, query_context, collapsed_state, float(probabilities[collapsed_idx])))
        self.conn.commit()
        
        # 更新缓存（坍缩后变为纯态）
        new_amplitude = np.zeros_like(qk.amplitude)
        new_amplitude[collapsed_idx] = 1.0
        qk.amplitude = new_amplitude
        qk.last_observation = collapsed_state
        
        # 触发纠缠态的瞬时关联（非局域性）
        self._update_entangled_states(crystal_id, collapsed_idx)
        
        return {
            'crystal_id': crystal_id,
            'collapsed_to': collapsed_state,
            'probability': float(probabilities[collapsed_idx]),
            'coherence_broken': True,
            'alternatives': [
                {'state': state, 'probability': float(prob)}
                for state, prob in zip(qk.basis_states, probabilities)
            ]
        }
    
    def _build_observable_from_context(self, qk: KnowledgeQubit, context: str) -> np.ndarray:
        """
        从查询上下文构建观测算符（厄米矩阵）
        例如：上下文含"高性能"倾向Implementation B（索引1）
        """
        n = len(qk.basis_states)
        observable = np.eye(n) * 0.5  # 默认单位矩阵（中性观测）
        
        # 基于关键词调整投影权重
        keywords_bias = {
            '高性能': 1,      # 倾向索引1（假设是高性能方案）
            '低成本': 0,      # 倾向索引0（低成本方案）
            '灵活': 2,        # 倾向索引2（混合方案）
            '实时': 1,
            '离线': 0
        }
        
        for keyword, bias_idx in keywords_bias.items():
            if keyword in context:
                # 增加该基态的观测权重（投影算符）
                observable[bias_idx, bias_idx] += 0.3
        
        return observable
    
    def entangle(self, crystal_a: str, crystal_b: str, strength: float = 0.9):
        """
        建立量子纠缠：两个知识晶体关联
        应用场景：概念-实现纠缠，问题-解决方案纠缠
        """
        # 贝尔态构建（简化：最大纠缠 |Φ+⟩ = (|00⟩+|11⟩)/√2 的推广）
        qa = self._load_qubit(crystal_a)
        qb = self._load_qubit(crystal_b)
        
        if qa is None or qb is None:
            return {'error': 'One or both qubits not found'}
        
        # 记录纠缠关系
        pair_id = f"ENT-{hash(tuple(sorted([crystal_a, crystal_b]))) % 10000:04d}"
        self.conn.execute("""
            INSERT OR REPLACE INTO entanglement_registry 
            (pair_id, crystal_a, crystal_b, entanglement_strength, created_at)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, datetime('now'))
        """, (pair_id, crystal_a, crystal_b, strength))
        
        # 更新各自的纠缠集合
        qa.entangled_with.add(crystal_b)
        qb.entangled_with.add(crystal_a)
        
        self.conn.execute("""
            UPDATE quantum_states SET entangled_with = ? WHERE crystal_id = ?
        """, (json.dumps(list(qa.entangled_with)), crystal_a))
        self.conn.execute("""
            UPDATE quantum_states SET entangled_with = ? WHERE crystal_id = ?
        """, (json.dumps(list(qb.entangled_with)), crystal_b))
        self.conn.commit()
        
        return {
            'entanglement_id': pair_id,
            'strength': strength,
            'type': 'maximal' if strength > 0.9 else 'partial'
        }
    
    def _update_entangled_states(self, observed_id: str, collapsed_idx: int):
        """
        纠缠态更新：对一个的观测瞬间确定另一个的状态
        量子非局域性在知识图谱中的体现
        """
        qk = self._load_qubit(observed_id)
        if not qk or not qk.entangled_with:
            return
        
        for entangled_id in qk.entangled_with:
            eq = self._load_qubit(entangled_id)
            if eq is None:
                continue
            
            # 关联坍缩（简化：相同索引坍缩）
            # 实际应基于纠缠类型（贝尔态）计算
            new_amp = np.zeros_like(eq.amplitude)
            new_amp[collapsed_idx] = 1.0
            eq.amplitude = new_amp
            
            # 记录关联观测
            self.conn.execute("""
                INSERT INTO observation_history 
                (crystal_id, query_context, collapsed_state, probability, observation_time, observer_signature)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, datetime('now'), 'entanglement_collapse')
            """, (entangled_id, f"entangled_with:{observed_id}", 
                  eq.basis_states[collapsed_idx], 1.0))
        
        self.conn.commit()
    
    def _load_qubit(self, crystal_id: str) -> Optional[KnowledgeQubit]:
        """从数据库加载量子态"""
        if crystal_id in self.hilbert_cache:
            return self.hilbert_cache[crystal_id]
        
        cursor = self.conn.execute("""
            SELECT amplitude_real, amplitude_imag, basis_states, entangled_with, coherence_ttl
            FROM quantum_states WHERE crystal_id = ?
        """, (crystal_id,))
        
        row = cursor.fetchone()
        if not row:
            return None
        
        real = json.loads(row[0])
        imag = json.loads(row[1])
        amp = np.array([complex(r, i) for r, i in zip(real, imag)])
        
        qk = KnowledgeQubit(
            crystal_id=crystal_id,
            amplitude=amp,
            basis_states=json.loads(row[2]),
            entangled_with=set(json.loads(row[3])) if row[3] else set(),
            coherence_time=row[4],
            last_observation=None
        )
        
        self.hilbert_cache[crystal_id] = qk
        return qk
    
    def quantum_interference(self, crystal_id: str, 
                            alternative_path_a: int, 
                            alternative_path_b: int) -> float:
        """
        量子干涉：计算两条认知路径的干涉项
        建设性干涉（+）增强概率，破坏性干涉（-）抑制概率
        """
        qk = self._load_qubit(crystal_id)
        if qk is None or len(qk.amplitude) <= max(alternative_path_a, alternative_path_b):
            return 0.0
        
        amp_a = qk.amplitude[alternative_path_a]
        amp_b = qk.amplitude[alternative_path_b]
        
        # 干涉项 2Re(α_a* α_b)
        interference = 2 * (amp_a.conj() * amp_b).real
        
        return float(interference)
    
    def get_quantum_stats(self) -> Dict:
        """量子知识统计"""
        cursor = self.conn.execute("""
            SELECT COUNT(*), AVG(length(amplitude_real)) 
            FROM quantum_states
        """)
        total, avg_dims = cursor.fetchone()
        
        cursor = self.conn.execute("""
            SELECT COUNT(*) FROM entanglement_registry
        """)
        entanglements = cursor.fetchone()[0]
        
        cursor = self.conn.execute("""
            SELECT collapsed_state, COUNT(*) 
            FROM observation_history 
            WHERE observation_time > date('now', '-7 days')
            GROUP BY collapsed_state
        """)
        observation_stats = {row[0]: row[1] for row in cursor.fetchall()}
        
        return {
            'total_superpositions': total,
            'average_dimensions': avg_dims,
            'active_entanglements': entanglements,
            'observation_frequency_7d': sum(observation_stats.values()),
            'popular_collapsed_states': observation_stats
        }

# 验证代码
def verify_quantum_knowledge():
    """验证量子知识系统"""
    import tempfile
    import shutil
    
    temp_dir = tempfile.mkdtemp()
    db_path = f"{temp_dir}/qks.db"
    
    try:
        qks = QuantumKnowledgeSpace(db_path, dim=3)
        
        # 测试1：创建叠加态
        implementations = [
            {'name': '规则引擎', 'content': '基于硬编码规则的匹配系统'},
            {'name': '神经网络', 'content': '基于深度学习的预测模型'},
            {'name': '混合方案', 'content': '规则+ML的混合架构'}
        ]
        
        qk = qks.create_superposition('PartnerMatcher-Arch', implementations)
        assert len(qk.amplitude) == 3, "叠加态维度错误"
        assert abs(np.sum(np.abs(qk.amplitude)**2) - 1.0) < 0.01, "概率幅未归一化"
        
        # 测试2：观测坍缩
        # 查询上下文暗示"高性能"，应倾向神经网络
        result = qks.observe('PartnerMatcher-Arch', '需要高性能实时匹配系统')
        collapsed = result['collapsed_to']
        prob = result['probability']
        
        assert collapsed in ['规则引擎', '神经网络', '混合方案'], "坍缩到未知态"
        assert 0 <= prob <= 1, "概率越界"
        print(f"   观测坍缩: {collapsed} (概率{prob:.2%})")
        
        # 测试3：纠缠建立
        # 创建另一个相关知识的叠加态
        qks.create_superposition('DataStore-Arch', [
            {'name': 'SQL数据库', 'content': '关系型存储'},
            {'name': 'NoSQL', 'content': '文档存储'},
            {'name': '混合存储', 'content': 'SQL+NoSQL'}
        ])
        
        ent_result = qks.entangle('PartnerMatcher-Arch', 'DataStore-Arch', strength=0.95)
        assert ent_result['entanglement_id'], "纠缠建立失败"
        assert ent_result['strength'] == 0.95, "纠缠强度错误"
        
        # 测试4：纠缠非局域性
        # 观测PartnerMatcher应瞬间影响DataStore
        result2 = qks.observe('PartnerMatcher-Arch', '选择规则引擎方案')
        # 检查DataStore是否也坍缩（在纠缠更新后）
        
        print("✅ 量子知识系统验证通过")
        print(f"   - 叠加态: {len(qk.amplitude)}维概率幅")
        print(f"   - 坍缩: →{collapsed} (上下文:'高性能')")
        print(f"   - 纠缠: {ent_result['entanglement_id']} (强度{ent_result['strength']})")
        print(f"   - 干涉项: {qks.quantum_interference('PartnerMatcher-Arch', 0, 1):.3f}")
        
        return True
        
    finally:
        shutil.rmtree(temp_dir, ignore_errors=True)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    verify_quantum_knowledge()

## 第十五轮洞察：耗散结构理论——知识系统的熵减与负熵流

### 第一性原理洞察

普利高津的耗散结构：开放系统在远离平衡态时，通过与外界交换物质/能量/信息，可能形成有序结构（负熵）。知识系统若封闭则熵增（混乱度增加），必须建立持续的知识代谢（流入高质量新知，排出陈旧认知）。

### 方法论：Dissipative Knowledge Structure (DKS)

建立熵的定量计算： - 知识熵：基于信息论香农熵，衡量知识库的无序度 - 负熵流：新验证知识（高确定性）作为负熵输入 - 耗散阈值：当熵产率超过临界值，触发知识相变（重组）

### 技术选型理由

为何用香农熵而非简单计数：熵同时考虑知识分布的均匀性（到处散落无重点=高熵）和确定性（验证状态）。

为何需要相变机制：当系统混乱度超过临界，局部优化无效，必须全局重构（类似水→冰的相变）。

### 代码级实施方案

# dissipative_structure/entropy_manager.py
# 耗散结构理论：知识熵、负熵流与相变重组

import sqlite3
import math
import numpy as np
from typing import Dict, List, Tuple, Optional
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from collections import Counter

@dataclass
class EntropyProfile:
    """知识系统熵画像"""
    total_entropy: float           # 总熵值（比特）
    entropy_density: float         # 熵密度（熵/知识晶体数）
    negentropy_flow: float         # 负熵流速率（比特/天）
    dissipation_rate: float        # 耗散率（新陈代谢谢率）
    distance_from_equilibrium: float  # 距平衡态距离（0=死亡，1=活跃）
    phase_transition_risk: str     # 相变风险等级

class DissipativeKnowledgeSystem:
    """
    耗散结构知识系统：基于普利高津理论管理知识熵
    核心：开放系统通过与外界交换维持低熵有序态
    """
    
    # 耗散结构临界参数
    CRITICAL_ENTROPY_PER_CRYSTAL = 2.0  # 每个晶体超过2比特熵触发警戒
    NEGENTROPY_INPUT_THRESHOLD = 10.0    # 每日需至少10比特负熵输入
    PHASE_TRANSITION_THRESHOLD = 0.7   # 熵产率超过70%触发相变
    
    def __init__(self, crystal_db: str, db_path: str = "dissipative_structure/entropy.db"):
        self.crystal_db = crystal_db
        self.conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path)
        self._init_entropy_schema()
    
    def _init_entropy_schema(self):
        self.conn.executescript("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS entropy_history (
                date TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                total_entropy REAL,
                crystal_count INTEGER,
                verified_crystals INTEGER,
                new_input_count INTEGER,
                deprecated_count INTEGER,
                negentropy_flow REAL,
                entropy_production REAL
            );
            
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS negentropy_sources (
                source_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                source_type TEXT,  -- 'external_validation', 'user_feedback', 'auto_verification'
                entropy_reduction REAL,  -- 负熵值（比特）
                timestamp TIMESTAMP,
                crystal_id TEXT
            );
            
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS phase_transition_log (
                transition_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                trigger_time TIMESTAMP,
                pre_entropy REAL,
                post_entropy REAL,
                reorganization_type TEXT,  -- 'compaction', 'hierarchy_rebuild', 'cold_storage_flush'
                affected_crystals INTEGER
            );
        """)
        self.conn.commit()
    
    def calculate_shannon_entropy(self, crystal_id: Optional[str] = None) -> float:
        """
        计算香农熵：H = -Σ p_i log₂(p_i)
        知识角度：p_i为晶体处于不同状态的概率分布
        """
        if crystal_id:
            # 单个晶体的熵（基于验证历史和引用分布）
            return self._single_crystal_entropy(crystal_id)
        
        # 系统总熵（基于知识分布的宏观状态）
        return self._system_entropy()
    
    def _single_crystal_entropy(self, crystal_id: str) -> float:
        """单个晶体的熵（不确定性度量）"""
        # 连接主知识库
        crystal_conn = sqlite3.connect(self.crystal_db)
        
        # 获取验证历史（二态：验证/未验证）
        cursor = crystal_conn.execute("""
            SELECT verify_result, COUNT(*) FROM verification_logs
            WHERE crystal_id = ? GROUP BY verify_result
        """, (crystal_id,))
        
        verifications = cursor.fetchall()
        crystal_conn.close()
        
        if not verifications:
            return 1.0  # 最大熵（完全未知）
        
        total = sum(v[1] for v in verifications)
        probabilities = [v[1]/total for v in verifications]
        
        # 香农熵计算
        entropy = -sum(p * math.log2(p) for p in probabilities if p > 0)
        
        # 加上时间衰减不确定性（越旧熵越高）
        cursor = self.conn.execute("""
            SELECT julianday('now') - julianday(last_verified_at) 
            FROM crystal_awareness WHERE crystal_id = ?
        """, (crystal_id,))
        row = cursor.fetchone()
        if row and row[0]:
            time_decay = min(row[0] / 365, 1.0)  # 一年内归一化
            entropy += time_decay * 0.5  # 时间不确定性加成
        
        return entropy
    
    def _system_entropy(self) -> float:
        """系统宏观熵（基于知识分布）"""
        # 1. 空间分布熵（按模块/分类的分布均匀性）
        cursor = self.conn.execute(f"""
            SELECT source_level, COUNT(*) FROM {self.crystal_db}.knowledge_crystals
            GROUP BY source_level
        """)
        
        level_dist = cursor.fetchall()
        total = sum(x[1] for x in level_dist)
        if total == 0:
            return 0.0
        
        probabilities = [x[1]/total for x in level_dist]
        spatial_entropy = -sum(p * math.log2(p) for p in probabilities if p > 0)
        
        # 2. 时间分布熵（知识年龄的分布）
        cursor = self.conn.execute(f"""
            SELECT strftime('%Y-%m', created_at) as month, COUNT(*)
            FROM {self.crystal_db}.knowledge_crystals
            GROUP BY month ORDER BY month DESC LIMIT 12
        """)
        
        time_dist = cursor.fetchall()
        if time_dist:
            total_time = sum(x[1] for x in time_dist)
            time_probs = [x[1]/total_time for x in time_dist]
            temporal_entropy = -sum(p * math.log2(p) for p in time_probs if p > 0)
        else:
            temporal_entropy = 0
        
        # 3. 验证状态熵（verified/unverified/cold的比例）
        cursor = self.conn.execute(f"""
            SELECT status, COUNT(*) FROM {self.crystal_db}.knowledge_crystals
            GROUP BY status
        """)
        
        status_dist = cursor.fetchall()
        total_status = sum(x[1] for x in status_dist)
        if total_status > 0:
            status_probs = [x[1]/total_status for x in status_dist]
            status_entropy = -sum(p * math.log2(p) for p in status_probs if p > 0)
        else:
            status_entropy = 0
        
        # 加权总熵（空间均匀性权重最高，避免知识散乱）
        total_entropy = spatial_entropy * 0.5 + temporal_entropy * 0.3 + status_entropy * 0.2
        
        return total_entropy
    
    def calculate_negentropy_flow(self, days: int = 7) -> float:
        """
        计算负熵流：系统通过验证、反馈、优化引入的秩序度
        负熵 = 验证带来的信息增益 + 淘汰混乱知识移除的熵
        """
        since = datetime.now() - timedelta(days=days)
        
        # 1. 验证引入的负熵（每个验证减少不确定性）
        cursor = self.conn.execute("""
            SELECT COUNT(*) * 1.5 as verification_negentropy
            FROM negentropy_sources
            WHERE timestamp > ? AND source_type = 'external_validation'
        """, (since,))
        
        verification_negentropy = cursor.fetchone()[0] or 0
        
        # 2. 淘汰移除的熵（清理混乱）
        cursor = self.conn.execute(f"""
            SELECT COUNT(*) * 2.0 as purge_negentropy
            FROM {self.crystal_db}.knowledge_crystals
            WHERE status = 'deprecated' AND last_verified_at > ?
        """, (since,))
        
        purge_negentropy = cursor.fetchone()[0] or 0
        
        # 3. 结构重组带来的负熵（压缩、索引优化）
        cursor = self.conn.execute("""
            SELECT SUM(entropy_reduction) FROM negentropy_sources
            WHERE timestamp > ? AND source_type = 'structure_optimization'
        """, (since,))
        
        structure_negentropy = cursor.fetchone()[0] or 0
        
        total_negentropy = verification_negentropy + purge_negentropy + structure_negentropy
        
        # 日均负熵流
        daily_flow = total_negentropy / days
        
        return daily_flow
    
    def check_phase_transition(self) -> Dict:
        """
        检查是否需要知识相变（全局重组）
        触发条件：熵产率 > 阈值 或 距平衡态太近（死寂）
        """
        # 当前熵态
        current_entropy = self._system_entropy()
        
        # 晶体总数
        cursor = self.conn.execute(f"""
            SELECT COUNT(*) FROM {self.crystal_db}.knowledge_crystals
        """)
        crystal_count = cursor.fetchone()[0]
        
        if crystal_count == 0:
            return {'risk': 'none', 'entropy_per_crystal': 0}
        
        entropy_per_crystal = current_entropy / crystal_count
        
        # 计算熵产率（最近7天熵增速度）
        cursor = self.conn.execute("""
            SELECT (total_entropy - LAG(total_entropy) OVER (ORDER BY date)) / 7
            FROM entropy_history
            ORDER BY date DESC LIMIT 7
        """)
        
        entropy_production_rates = [row[0] for row in cursor.fetchall() if row[0] is not None]
        avg_production = np.mean(entropy_production_rates) if entropy_production_rates else 0
        
        # 距平衡态距离（disequilibrium measure）
        # 理想状态：适度有序（非零熵也非最大熵）
        max_entropy = math.log2(max(crystal_count, 2))  # 最大可能熵
        distance_from_eq = 1.0 - abs(current_entropy - max_entropy/2) / (max_entropy/2)
        
        # 相变决策
        risk = 'low'
        action = 'maintain'
        
        if entropy_per_crystal > self.CRITICAL_ENTROPY_PER_CRYSTAL:
            risk = 'critical'
            action = 'emergency_reorganization'
        elif avg_production > self.PHASE_TRANSITION_THRESHOLD:
            risk = 'high'
            action = 'scheduled_reorganization'
        elif distance_from_eq < 0.3:
            risk = 'medium'
            action = 'stimulation_needed'  # 需要引入新负熵
        
        return {
            'risk_level': risk,
            'entropy_per_crystal': entropy_per_crystal,
            'entropy_production_rate': avg_production,
            'distance_from_equilibrium': distance_from_eq,
            'recommended_action': action,
            'crystal_count': crystal_count
        }
    
    def execute_phase_transition(self, transition_type: str = 'compaction') -> Dict:
        """
        执行知识相变：全局重组以降低系统熵
        类似文件系统的碎片整理，但针对知识结构
        """
        start_entropy = self._system_entropy()
        affected = 0
        
        if transition_type == 'compaction':
            # 压缩：合并相似知识（SimHash接近的晶体合并）
            affected = self._compaction_reorganization()
        elif transition_type == 'hierarchy_rebuild':
            # 层级重建：基于引用关系重新聚类
            affected = self._hierarchy_rebuild()
        elif transition_type == 'cold_flush':
            # 冷存储刷新：将高熵（混乱）知识移入深度冷冻
            affected = self._cold_storage_flush()
        
        end_entropy = self._system_entropy()
        
        # 记录相变
        trans_id = f"PT-{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d%H%M')}"
        self.conn.execute("""
            INSERT INTO phase_transition_log 
            (transition_id, trigger_time, pre_entropy, post_entropy, 
             reorganization_type, affected_crystals)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
        """, (trans_id, datetime.now(), start_entropy, end_entropy, 
              transition_type, affected))
        self.conn.commit()
        
        return {
            'transition_id': trans_id,
            'entropy_reduction': start_entropy - end_entropy,
            'affected_crystals': affected,
            'efficiency': (start_entropy - end_entropy) / max(affected, 1)
        }
    
    def _compaction_reorganization(self) -> int:
        """压缩重组：合并高相似度晶体"""
        # 查找SimHash海明距离<3的晶体对
        # 简化实现：返回模拟值
        return 5
    
    def _hierarchy_rebuild(self) -> int:
        """层级重建"""
        return 3
    
    def _cold_storage_flush(self) -> int:
        """高熵知识冷冻"""
        # 将熵值>2.0且长期未验证的知识移入深度存储
        return 2
    
    def record_daily_entropy(self):
        """记录每日熵态（由daily_asset_runner调用）"""
        today = datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d')
        
        total_entropy = self._system_entropy()
        
        cursor = self.conn.execute(f"""
            SELECT COUNT(*) FROM {self.crystal_db}.knowledge_crystals
        """)
        crystal_count = cursor.fetchone()[0]
        
        cursor = self.conn.execute(f"""
            SELECT COUNT(*) FROM {self.crystal_db}.knowledge_crystals WHERE status='verified'
        """)
        verified = cursor.fetchone()[0]
        
        cursor = self.conn.execute(f"""
            SELECT COUNT(*) FROM {self.crystal_db}.knowledge_crystals 
            WHERE created_at > date('now', '-1 day')
        """)
        new_input = cursor.fetchone()[0]
        
        cursor = self.conn.execute(f"""
            SELECT COUNT(*) FROM {self.crystal_db}.knowledge_crystals 
            WHERE status='deprecated' AND last_verified_at > date('now', '-1 day')
        """)
        deprecated = cursor.fetchone()[0]
        
        negentropy = self.calculate_negentropy_flow(1)
        
        self.conn.execute("""
            INSERT OR REPLACE INTO entropy_history 
            (date, total_entropy, crystal_count, verified_crystals, new_input_count,
             deprecated_count, negentropy_flow, entropy_production)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
        """, (today, total_entropy, crystal_count, verified, new_input, 
              deprecated, negentropy, 0))  # entropy_production需历史数据计算
        self.conn.commit()
    
    def get_dissipative_report(self) -> Dict:
        """耗散结构健康报告"""
        phase_check = self.check_phase_transition()
        negentropy = self.calculate_negentropy_flow(7)
        
        return {
            'system_status': phase_check['risk_level'],
            'entropy_metrics': {
                'total_bits': self._system_entropy(),
                'per_crystal': phase_check['entropy_per_crystal'],
                'critical_threshold': self.CRITICAL_ENTROPY_PER_CRYSTAL
            },
            'thermodynamics': {
                'negentropy_flow_7d': negentropy,
                'required_minimum': self.NEGENTROPY_INPUT_THRESHOLD,
                'is_open_system': negentropy > self.NEGENTROPY_INPUT_THRESHOLD * 0.5
            },
            'phase_transition': phase_check,
            'recommendation': 'Input more external validations' if negentropy < self.NEGENTROPY_INPUT_THRESHOLD else 'System is healthy'
        }

# 验证代码
def verify_dissipative_system():
    """验证耗散结构系统"""
    import tempfile
    import shutil
    
    temp_dir = tempfile.mkdtemp()
    crystal_db = f"{temp_dir}/crystal.db"
    entropy_db = f"{temp_dir}/entropy.db"
    
    # 创建模拟知识库
    conn = sqlite3.connect(crystal_db)
    conn.executescript("""
        CREATE TABLE knowledge_crystals (crystal_id TEXT, status TEXT, created_at TIMESTAMP, source_level TEXT);
        CREATE TABLE verification_logs (crystal_id TEXT, verify_result TEXT);
    """)
    # 插入测试数据
    for i in range(10):
        conn.execute("INSERT INTO knowledge_crystals VALUES (?, 'active', datetime('now'), ?)",
                    (f'crystal_{i}', 'S' if i < 3 else 'A' if i < 7 else 'B'))
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()
    
    try:
        dks = DissipativeKnowledgeSystem(crystal_db, entropy_db)
        
        # 测试1：熵计算
        entropy = dks._system_entropy()
        assert entropy >= 0, "熵不应为负"
        print(f"   系统熵: {entropy:.2f} bits")
        
        # 测试2：负熵流（初始应为0）
        neg_flow = dks.calculate_negentropy_flow(7)
        assert neg_flow >= 0, "负熵流不应为负"
        
        # 测试3：插入负熵源并重新计算
        dks.conn.execute("""
            INSERT INTO negentropy_sources VALUES 
            ('val_001', 'external_validation', 2.5, datetime('now'), 'crystal_0')
        """)
        dks.conn.commit()
        
        neg_flow2 = dks.calculate_negentropy_flow(7)
        assert neg_flow2 > neg_flow, "负熵流应增加"
        
        # 测试4：相变检查
        phase = dks.check_phase_transition()
        assert 'risk_level' in phase, "相变检查失败"
        
        # 测试5：相变执行
        if phase['risk_level'] in ['critical', 'high']:
            result = dks.execute_phase_transition('compaction')
            assert 'entropy_reduction' in result, "相变执行失败"
            print(f"   相变执行: 熵减{result['entropy_reduction']:.2f} bits")
        
        print("✅ 耗散结构系统验证通过")
        print(f"   - 系统熵: {entropy:.2f} bits")
        print(f"   - 负熵流: {neg_flow2:.2f} bits/day")
        print(f"   - 相变风险: {phase['risk_level']}")
        print(f"   - 距平衡态: {phase['distance_from_equilibrium']:.2f}")
        
        return True
        
    finally:
        shutil.rmtree(temp_dir, ignore_errors=True)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    verify_dissipative_system()

## 第十六轮洞察：因果推断层——反事实推理与do-calculus

### 第一性原理洞察

相关性≠因果性。当前系统基于相似性检索（向量接近），但决策需要因果推理（“如果采用方案A而非B，结果会如何”）。必须建立因果图谱（Causal Graph），支持Pearl的do-演算（do-calculus）。

### 方法论：Structural Causal Model (SCM) for Knowledge

建立三层因果： 1. 观察层：P(Y|X)（现有数据的相关性） 2. 干预层：P(Y|do(X))（强制实施X后的结果） 3. 反事实层：P(Y_x|X’,Y’)（已经做了X’得到Y’，如果当时做X会如何）

### 技术选型理由

为何用前门准则而非后门：知识系统中常有不可观测的混杂因子（满意姐的主观判断），前门准则更适合。

为何本地实现do-calculus：通过倾向得分匹配（Propensity Score Matching）在SQLite内实现干预模拟，无需外部因果推理引擎。

### 代码级实施方案

# causal_inference/causal_knowledge_engine.py
# 因果推断引擎：do-calculus与反事实推理

import sqlite3
import numpy as np
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Tuple, Set
from dataclasses import dataclass
from collections import defaultdict
import json
import random

@dataclass
class CausalEffect:
    """因果效应估计"""
    ate: float          # Average Treatment Effect（平均处理效应）
    confidence_interval: Tuple[float, float]
    p_value: float
    method: str         # 'backdoor', 'frontdoor', 'iv'（工具变量）
    assumptions: List[str]  # 识别假设

class CausalKnowledgeEngine:
    """
    因果知识引擎：在知识图谱上执行因果推断
    回答"如果...会怎样"（反事实问题）
    """
    
    def __init__(self, db_path: str = "causal_inference/causal.db"):
        self.conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path)
        self._init_causal_schema()
        self.graph = self._build_causal_graph()
    
    def _init_causal_schema(self):
        self.conn.executescript("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS causal_graph (
                edge_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                from_node TEXT,
                to_node TEXT,
                edge_type TEXT,  -- 'direct', 'confounding', 'mediator', 'collider'
                strength REAL,     -- 因果强度（偏回归系数）
                evidence_count INTEGER
            );
            
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS interventions (
                intervention_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                target_variable TEXT,
                do_value TEXT,
                context TEXT,
                estimated_outcome REAL,
                actual_outcome REAL,
                counterfactual_outcome REAL,  -- 反事实：如果不干预的结果
                timestamp TIMESTAMP
            );
            
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS counterfactual_log (
                cf_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                factual_scenario TEXT,    -- 实际发生的情况
                counterfactual_scenario TEXT,  -- 假设的情况
                node_id TEXT,
                factual_value TEXT,
                counterfactual_value TEXT,
                estimated_effect REAL
            );
        """)
        self.conn.commit()
    
    def _build_causal_graph(self) -> Dict[str, Set[str]]:
        """从数据库构建因果图（有向无环图DAG）"""
        cursor = self.conn.execute("""
            SELECT from_node, to_node FROM causal_graph WHERE edge_type = 'direct'
        """)
        
        graph = defaultdict(set)
        for row in cursor.fetchall():
            graph[row[0]].add(row[1])
        
        return dict(graph)
    
    def identify_backdoor_paths(self, treatment: str, outcome: str) -> List[List[str]]:
        """
        识别后门路径：T ← Z → Y（混杂路径）
        这些路径需要被阻断（conditioning）以估计因果效应
        """
        # 简化实现：查找所有从treatment到outcome的非因果路径
        # 实际应使用图算法（BFS/DFS）查找经过混杂节点的路径
        paths = []
        
        # 查找直接指向treatment和outcome的共同原因（混杂因子）
        cursor = self.conn.execute("""
            SELECT cg1.from_node 
            FROM causal_graph cg1
            JOIN causal_graph cg2 ON cg1.from_node = cg2.from_node
            WHERE cg1.to_node = ? AND cg2.to_node = ? AND cg1.edge_type = 'confounding'
        """, (treatment, outcome))
        
        for row in cursor.fetchall():
            confounder = row[0]
            paths.append([treatment, confounder, outcome])
        
        return paths
    
    def estimate_ate(self, treatment: str, outcome: str, 
                    adjustment_set: Optional[List[str]] = None) -> CausalEffect:
        """
        估计平均处理效应（Average Treatment Effect）
        ATE = E[Y|do(T=1)] - E[Y|do(T=0)]
        使用后门准则调整
        """
        if adjustment_set is None:
            # 自动识别调整集（所有后门路径的混杂因子）
            backdoors = self.identify_backdoor_paths(treatment, outcome)
            adjustment_set = list(set(node for path in backdoors for node in path 
                                     if node not in [treatment, outcome]))
        
        # 从知识库获取数据（模拟）
        # 实际应从crystal_knowledge_graph提取相关决策记录
        data = self._fetch_decision_data(treatment, outcome, adjustment_set)
        
        if len(data) < 10:
            return CausalEffect(
                ate=0.0,
                confidence_interval=(0, 0),
                p_value=1.0,
                method='insufficient_data',
                assumptions=['more_data_needed']
            )
        
        # 分层调整（Stratification）估计ATE
        treated_outcomes = []
        control_outcomes = []
        
        for record in data:
            if record['treatment'] == 1:
                treated_outcomes.append(record['outcome'])
            else:
                control_outcomes.append(record['outcome'])
        
        ate = np.mean(treated_outcomes) - np.mean(control_outcomes)
        
        # 置信区间（Bootstrap）
        bootstrap_ates = []
        for _ in range(1000):
            sample_t = np.random.choice(treated_outcomes, len(treated_outcomes), replace=True)
            sample_c = np.random.choice(control_outcomes, len(control_outcomes), replace=True)
            bootstrap_ates.append(np.mean(sample_t) - np.mean(sample_c))
        
        ci_lower = np.percentile(bootstrap_ates, 2.5)
        ci_upper = np.percentile(bootstrap_ates, 97.5)
        
        return CausalEffect(
            ate=ate,
            confidence_interval=(ci_lower, ci_upper),
            p_value=0.05 if 0 not in (ci_lower, ci_upper) else 0.5,
            method='backdoor_adjustment',
            assumptions=[f'adjust_for:{adjustment_set}', 'no_unobserved_confounding']
        )
    
    def _fetch_decision_data(self, treatment: str, outcome: str, 
                            adjustment_set: List[str]) -> List[Dict]:
        """获取决策历史数据（模拟）"""
        # 模拟数据：实际应从verification_logs等表提取
        return [
            {'treatment': 1, 'outcome': 0.8, 'confounder': 'high_skill'},
            {'treatment': 0, 'outcome': 0.4, 'confounder': 'low_skill'},
        ] * 20  # 模拟20条记录
    
    def counterfactual_query(self, factual_evidence: Dict, 
                           intervention: Dict) -> Dict:
        """
        反事实查询：如果当时采取不同行动，结果会如何？
        Pearl's three steps:
        1. Abduction（溯因）：基于证据更新P(U|e)
        2. Action（干预）：do(X=x)
        3. Prediction（预测）：计算P(Y=y|do(X=x), e)
        """
        # 步骤1：确定外生变量（简化：假设已知混杂因子）
        observed_confounders = factual_evidence.get('confounders', {})
        
        # 步骤2：干预（移除指向干预变量的入边，固定其值）
        intervention_var = list(intervention.keys())[0]
        intervention_value = intervention[intervention_var]
        
        # 步骤3：预测（基于结构调整公式）
        # 模拟：查找历史相似案例（倾向得分匹配）
        similar_cases = self._find_similar_cases(observed_confounders, 
                                               intervention_var, 
                                               intervention_value)
        
        if not similar_cases:
            return {
                'counterfactual_outcome': None,
                'confidence': 0,
                'note': 'No similar historical cases found'
            }
        
        # 估计反事实结果
        predicted_outcome = np.mean([case['outcome'] for case in similar_cases])
        factual_outcome = factual_evidence.get('actual_outcome', 0)
        
        # 个体处理效应（ITE）
        ite = predicted_outcome - factual_outcome
        
        return {
            'factual_outcome': factual_outcome,
            'counterfactual_outcome': predicted_outcome,
            'individual_treatment_effect': ite,
            'confidence': len(similar_cases) / 100,  # 样本量置信度
            'supporting_cases': len(similar_cases),
            'interpretation': f"If had done {intervention}, outcome would likely be {predicted_outcome:.2f} instead of {factual_outcome:.2f}"
        }
    
    def _find_similar_cases(self, confounders: Dict, treatment_var: str, 
                           treatment_val) -> List[Dict]:
        """倾向得分匹配：找与当前情境相似但接受不同处理的案例"""
        # 简化：返回模拟数据
        return [{'outcome': random.uniform(0.6, 0.9)} for _ in range(15)]
    
    def suggest_intervention(self, target_outcome: str, 
                            current_state: Dict) -> List[Dict]:
        """
        建议干预：为达到目标结果，应该采取什么行动？
        逆向因果推理
        """
        # 查找所有指向outcome的直接原因
        cursor = self.conn.execute("""
            SELECT from_node, strength FROM causal_graph 
            WHERE to_node = ? AND edge_type = 'direct'
            ORDER BY strength DESC
        """, (target_outcome,))
        
        causes = cursor.fetchall()
        
        suggestions = []
        for cause, strength in causes:
            # 估计当前状态下实施该因果的效应
            effect = self.estimate_ate(cause, target_outcome)
            
            if effect.ate > 0:
                suggestions.append({
                    'intervention': cause,
                    'expected_lift': effect.ate,
                    'confidence_interval': effect.confidence_interval,
                    'rationale': f"Historical data shows {cause} increases {target_outcome} by {effect.ate:.2f}"
                })
        
        return suggestions[:3]  # Top 3建议
    
    def record_intervention(self, intervention: Dict, outcome: float):
        """记录实际干预结果（用于验证因果模型）"""
        pass

# 验证代码
def verify_causal_engine():
    """验证因果推断引擎"""
    import tempfile
    import shutil
    
    temp_dir = tempfile.mkdtemp()
    db_path = f"{temp_dir}/causal.db"
    
    try:
        cke = CausalKnowledgeEngine(db_path)
        
        # 建立因果图（模拟）
        cke.conn.executemany("""
            INSERT INTO causal_graph (from_node, to_node, edge_type, strength, evidence_count)
            VALUES (?, ?, 'direct', ?, 10)
        """, [
            ('使用HyperCompressor', 'Token节省', 0.8),
            ('外部API调用', '系统成本', -0.9),
            ('知识验证频率', '决策准确性', 0.7),
            ('团队经验', '使用HyperCompressor', 0.6),  # 混杂因子
            ('团队经验', 'Token节省', 0.5),
        ])
        cke.conn.commit()
        
        # 重新加载图
        cke.graph = cke._build_causal_graph()
        
        # 测试1：后门路径识别
        paths = cke.identify_backdoor_paths('使用HyperCompressor', 'Token节省')
        print(f"   发现{len(paths)}条后门路径（混杂路径）")
        
        # 测试2：ATE估计
        effect = cke.estimate_ate('使用HyperCompressor', 'Token节省', 
                                  adjustment_set=['团队经验'])
        print(f"   ATE估计: {effect.ate:.2f} (方法: {effect.method})")
        assert isinstance(effect.ate, (int, float)), "ATE应为数值"
        
        # 测试3：反事实查询
        factual = {
            'actual_outcome': 0.4,
            'confounders': {'团队经验': 'junior'},
            'action_taken': '未使用HyperCompressor'
        }
        cf_result = cke.counterfactual_query(factual, {'使用HyperCompressor': True})
        
        assert 'counterfactual_outcome' in cf_result, "反事实结果缺失"
        print(f"   反事实: 如果使用了压缩，结果将从{factual['actual_outcome']}变为{cf_result['counterfactual_outcome']:.2f}")
        
        # 测试4：干预建议
        suggestions = cke.suggest_intervention('决策准确性', {'当前准确率': 0.6})
        assert len(suggestions) > 0, "应提供干预建议"
        
        print("✅ 因果推断引擎验证通过")
        print(f"   - 混杂控制: 调整了{len(effect.assumptions)}个变量")
        print(f"   - 因果效应: {effect.ate:.2f} (95%CI: {effect.confidence_interval})")
        print(f"   - 反事实推理: ITE={cf_result['individual_treatment_effect']:.2f}")
        print(f"   - 干预建议: {len(suggestions)}条")
        
        return True
        
    finally:
        shutil.rmtree(temp_dir, ignore_errors=True)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    verify_causal_engine()

## 第十七轮洞察：多智能体共识——五路图腾的分布式拜占庭容错

### 第一性原理洞察

“五路图腾”（confucius/guanyin/simon等）本质上是多智能体系统。当多个Agent对同一决策给出不同判断时，需要共识机制（Consensus）而非简单多数投票。借鉴拜占庭容错（BFT）与联邦学习思想。

### 方法论：PBFT-inspired Multi-Agent Consensus

建立实用拜占庭容错共识： 1. 预准备（Pre-prepare）：提议者（如蓝军）提出决策草案 2. 准备（Prepare）：各图腾Agent独立验证并签名 3. 提交（Commit）：2/3多数（≥4/5路）通过则共识达成 4. 视图变更（View Change）：若主节点（蓝军）故障，满意姐接管

### 技术选型理由

为何用PBFT而非Raft：五路图腾可能”说谎”（拜占庭故障），Raft仅处理宕机故障，PBFT可处理恶意/错误判断。

为何5个节点：PBFT要求n≥3f+1（f为容错数），5节点可容2个故障，满足”五路”数量且容错最大化。

### 代码级实施方案

# multi_agent/consensus_engine.py
# 多智能体共识引擎：五路图腾的拜占庭容错共识

import sqlite3
import hashlib
import json
import time
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Set, Tuple
from dataclasses import dataclass
from enum import Enum
from datetime import datetime

class ConsensusPhase(Enum):
    IDLE = "idle"
    PRE_PREPARE = "pre_prepare"
    PREPARE = "prepare"
    COMMIT = "commit"
    COMMITTED = "committed"
    VIEW_CHANGE = "view_change"

class AgentRole(Enum):
    CONFUCIUS = "confucius"      # 儒家伦理
    GUANYIN = "guanyin"          # 慈悲/弱势关怀
    SIMON = "simon"              # 有限理性
    BLUE_ARMY = "blue_army"      # 审计/质疑
    SATISFACTORY = "satisfactory"  # 决策者（视图领导者）

@dataclass
class ConsensusMessage:
    """共识消息"""
    view_number: int          # 视图编号（类似Raft的term）
    sequence_number: int        # 序列号（决策ID）
    digest: str                 # 决策内容哈希
    phase: ConsensusPhase
    from_agent: AgentRole
    signature: str              # ECDSA签名（简化：哈希模拟）

class FiveWayConsensus:
    """
    五路图腾共识引擎：PBFT变体实现
    5个节点，容忍2个拜占庭故障（恶意/错误判断）
    """
    
    def __init__(self, db_path: str = "multi_agent/consensus.db"):
        self.conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path)
        self._init_consensus_schema()
        
        self.agents = set(AgentRole)
        self.f = 1  # 容错数（5节点可容1个拜占庭，实际理论是2，保守取1）
        self.quorum = 2 * self.f + 1  # 4个节点
        
        self.view_number = 0
        self.sequence_number = 0
        self.primary = AgentRole.BLUE_ARMY  # 默认蓝军为提议者
        
        self.message_log: Dict[Tuple[int, int], List[ConsensusMessage]] = {}
        self.prepared_certificates: Dict[int, Set[AgentRole]] = {}
        self.committed_certificates: Dict[int, Set[AgentRole]] = {}
    
    def _init_consensus_schema(self):
        self.conn.executescript("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS consensus_instances (
                sequence_number INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                view_number INTEGER,
                proposal_digest TEXT,
                proposal_content TEXT,
                status TEXT,
                decided_value TEXT,
                decided_at TIMESTAMP,
                participating_agents TEXT  -- JSON数组
            );
            
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS agent_votes (
                vote_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                sequence_number INTEGER,
                agent_name TEXT,
                phase TEXT,
                vote_digest TEXT,
                reasoning TEXT,  -- 各Agent的决策理由
                timestamp TIMESTAMP
            );
            
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS view_changes (
                view_number INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                old_primary TEXT,
                new_primary TEXT,
                reason TEXT,
                timestamp TIMESTAMP
            );
        """)
        self.conn.commit()
    
    def propose_decision(self, decision_content: str, 
                         proposer: AgentRole = AgentRole.BLUE_ARMY) -> int:
        """
        Pre-Prepare阶段：主节点提议决策
        类似蓝军审计报告提交给议会
        """
        if proposer != self.primary:
            raise ValueError(f"Only primary {self.primary} can propose")
        
        self.sequence_number += 1
        seq = self.sequence_number
        
        digest = hashlib.sha256(decision_content.encode()).hexdigest()[:16]
        
        # 记录提议
        self.conn.execute("""
            INSERT INTO consensus_instances 
            (sequence_number, view_number, proposal_digest, proposal_content, status)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
        """, (seq, self.view_number, digest, decision_content, 'pre_prepare'))
        self.conn.commit()
        
        # 创建Pre-Prepare消息
        pre_prepare_msg = ConsensusMessage(
            view_number=self.view_number,
            sequence_number=seq,
            digest=digest,
            phase=ConsensusPhase.PRE_PREPARE,
            from_agent=proposer,
            signature=f"SIG-{proposer.value}-{digest}"
        )
        
        # 广播给所有Agent（模拟）
        self._broadcast(pre_prepare_msg)
        
        # 自动进入Prepare阶段（各Agent独立验证）
        self._enter_prepare_phase(seq, digest, decision_content)
        
        return seq
    
    def _enter_prepare_phase(self, seq: int, digest: str, content: str):
        """Prepare阶段：各Agent独立验证提案"""
        votes = []
        
        for agent in self.agents:
            if agent == self.primary:
                continue  # 提议者已在Pre-Prepare阶段
            
            # 各Agent基于自身逻辑验证（模拟不同判断标准）
            is_valid, reasoning = self._agent_validate(agent, content)
            
            if is_valid:
                prepare_msg = ConsensusMessage(
                    view_number=self.view_number,
                    sequence_number=seq,
                    digest=digest,
                    phase=ConsensusPhase.PREPARE,
                    from_agent=agent,
                    signature=f"SIG-PREPARE-{agent.value}"
                )
                votes.append(prepare_msg)
                
                # 记录投票
                self.conn.execute("""
                    INSERT INTO agent_votes 
                    (sequence_number, agent_name, phase, vote_digest, reasoning, timestamp)
                    VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
                """, (seq, agent.value, 'prepare', digest, reasoning, datetime.now()))
        
        self.conn.commit()
        
        # 检查是否达成Prepare证书（2f个Prepare消息，加上Pre-Prepare共2f+1）
        self.prepared_certificates[seq] = set(v.from_agent for v in votes) | {self.primary}
        
        if len(self.prepared_certificates[seq]) >= self.quorum:
            self._enter_commit_phase(seq, digest)
    
    def _agent_validate(self, agent: AgentRole, content: str) -> Tuple[bool, str]:
        """模拟各Agent的独立验证逻辑"""
        # Confucius: 检查伦理合规
        if agent == AgentRole.CONFUCIUS:
            if '利益相关者' in content or '伦理' in content:
                return True, "符合儒家利益相关者平衡原则"
            return False, "缺乏伦理维度考量"
        
        # Guanyin: 检查弱势群体保护
        elif agent == AgentRole.GUANYIN:
            if '弱势群体' in content or '普惠' in content:
                return True, "体现慈悲心，关怀到位"
            return True, "无弱势群体负面影响"  # 默认通过
        
        # Simon: 检查有限理性约束
        elif agent == AgentRole.SIMON:
            if '信息完备' in content or '有限理性' in content:
                return True, "承认有限理性，决策边界清晰"
            return False, "假设完全理性，不符合西蒙框架"
        
        # Satisfactory: 业务可行性
        elif agent == AgentRole.SATISFACTORY:
            if '成本' in content and '收益' in content:
                return True, "经济效益可量化"
            return True, "战略价值优先"
        
        # Blue Army: 审计质疑（提议者，已在Pre-Prepare阶段验证）
        return True, "审计通过"
    
    def _enter_commit_phase(self, seq: int, digest: str):
        """Commit阶段：已Prepared的节点发送Commit"""
        prepared_agents = self.prepared_certificates[seq]
        
        for agent in prepared_agents:
            commit_msg = ConsensusMessage(
                view_number=self.view_number,
                sequence_number=seq,
                digest=digest,
                phase=ConsensusPhase.COMMIT,
                from_agent=agent,
                signature=f"SIG-COMMIT-{agent.value}"
            )
            
            self.conn.execute("""
                INSERT INTO agent_votes 
                (sequence_number, agent_name, phase, vote_digest, timestamp)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
            """, (seq, agent.value, 'commit', digest, datetime.now()))
        
        self.conn.commit()
        
        self.committed_certificates[seq] = prepared_agents
        
        if len(self.committed_certificates[seq]) >= self.quorum:
            self._finalize_consensus(seq, digest)
    
    def _finalize_consensus(self, seq: int, digest: str):
        """共识达成：提交最终决策"""
        cursor = self.conn.execute("""
            SELECT proposal_content FROM consensus_instances WHERE sequence_number = ?
        """, (seq,))
        
        content = cursor.fetchone()[0]
        
        self.conn.execute("""
            UPDATE consensus_instances 
            SET status = 'committed', decided_value = ?, decided_at = ?,
                participating_agents = ?
            WHERE sequence_number = ?
        """, (content, datetime.now(), 
              json.dumps([a.value for a in self.committed_certificates[seq]]),
              seq))
        self.conn.commit()
        
        print(f"✅ 共识达成 [Seq:{seq}] 五路参与: {[a.value for a in self.committed_certificates[seq]]}")
    
    def _broadcast(self, msg: ConsensusMessage):
        """模拟广播（实际应通过消息队列）"""
        key = (msg.view_number, msg.sequence_number)
        if key not in self.message_log:
            self.message_log[key] = []
        self.message_log[key].append(msg)
    
    def view_change(self, new_primary: AgentRole, reason: str):
        """视图变更：主节点故障时切换（如蓝军失效，满意姐接任）"""
        old_primary = self.primary
        self.primary = new_primary
        self.view_number += 1
        
        self.conn.execute("""
            INSERT INTO view_changes (view_number, old_primary, new_primary, reason, timestamp)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
        """, (self.view_number, old_primary.value, new_primary.value, reason, datetime.now()))
        self.conn.commit()
        
        print(f"🔄 视图变更: {old_primary.value} → {new_primary.value} (原因: {reason})")
    
    def check_consensus_status(self, seq: int) -> Dict:
        """查询共识状态"""
        cursor = self.conn.execute("""
            SELECT status, decided_value, participating_agents
            FROM consensus_instances WHERE sequence_number = ?
        """, (seq,))
        
        row = cursor.fetchone()
        if not row:
            return {'status': 'not_found'}
        
        return {
            'sequence_number': seq,
            'status': row[0],
            'decided': row[1],
            'participants': json.loads(row[2]) if row[2] else [],
            'view_number': self.view_number,
            'fault_tolerance': self.f
        }
    
    def get_consensus_history(self, limit: int = 10) -> List[Dict]:
        """获取历史共识记录"""
        cursor = self.conn.execute("""
            SELECT sequence_number, proposal_digest, status, decided_at, participating_agents
            FROM consensus_instances
            ORDER BY sequence_number DESC LIMIT ?
        """, (limit,))
        
        return [{
            'seq': row[0],
            'digest': row[1],
            'status': row[2],
            'decided_at': row[3],
            'agents': json.loads(row[4]) if row[4] else []
        } for row in cursor.fetchall()]

# 验证代码
def verify_consensus_engine():
    """验证五路共识引擎"""
    import tempfile
    import shutil
    
    temp_dir = tempfile.mkdtemp()
    db_path = f"{temp_dir}/consensus.db"
    
    try:
        consensus = FiveWayConsensus(db_path)
        
        # 测试1：正常共识流程
        decision = """
        决策：采用HyperCompressor方案优化Token消耗
        理由：基于本地n-gram哈希预过滤，减少90%LLM调用
        伦理：无弱势群体负面影响
        理性边界：承认启发式可能漏检，设置LLM兜底
        成本：开发成本20小时，节省月度Token费用5000元
        """
        
        seq = consensus.propose_decision(decision, AgentRole.BLUE_ARMY)
        
        # 检查状态
        status = consensus.check_consensus_status(seq)
        assert status['status'] in ['committed', 'pre_prepare'], f"共识状态异常: {status}"
        
        # 测试2：视图变更
        consensus.view_change(AgentRole.SATISFACTORY, "蓝军节点响应超时")
        assert consensus.primary == AgentRole.SATISFACTORY, "视图变更失败"
        
        # 测试3：在新视图下提议
        decision2 = "紧急决策：API超时fallback机制"
        seq2 = consensus.propose_decision(decision2, AgentRole.SATISFACTORY)
        
        status2 = consensus.check_consensus_status(seq2)
        
        print("✅ 五路共识引擎验证通过")
        print(f"   - 共识达成: Seq{seq}, 状态={status['status']}")
        print(f"   - 容错能力: 容忍{consensus.f}个拜占庭节点")
        print(f"   - 视图变更: {AgentRole.BLUE_ARMY.value}→{AgentRole.SATISFACTORY.value}")
        print(f"   - 历史记录: {len(consensus.get_consensus_history())}条")
        
        return True
        
    finally:
        shutil.rmtree(temp_dir, ignore_errors=True)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    verify_consensus_engine()

## 第十八轮洞察：反脆弱弹性——认知系统的压力测试与混沌工程

### 第一性原理洞察

塔勒布的反脆弱：系统不仅抵抗冲击，还从冲击中获益（变得更强）。当前系统被动防御，应建立主动混沌注入（Chaos Engineering），定期杀死组件、注入错误，训练系统的自愈与进化能力。

### 方法论：Chaos Monkey for Cognition

建立混沌注入-监控-恢复-强化循环： 1. 故障注入：随机删除知识晶体、模拟API超时、损坏索引 2. 弹性评估：测量恢复时间（MTTR）与数据完整性 3. 自愈强化：从故障中学习，生成更鲁棒的冗余机制 4. 疫苗更新：将故障模式纳入对抗训练（第十四轮）

### 代码级实施方案

# resilience/chaos_engine.py
# 混沌工程引擎：反脆弱认知系统的压力测试与自愈

import sqlite3
import random
import json
import time
import threading
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Callable
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from enum import Enum

class FaultType(Enum):
    """故障类型"""
    CRYSTAL_DELETE = "crystal_delete"      # 随机删除知识晶体
    INDEX_CORRUPTION = "index_corruption"  # 损坏检索索引
    API_TIMEOUT = "api_timeout"            # 模拟外部API超时
    NETWORK_PARTITION = "network_partition" # 网络分区（单机模拟为磁盘IO延迟）
    BIT_FLIP = "bit_flip"                  # 内存位翻转（模拟硬件故障）

@dataclass
class ChaosExperiment:
    """混沌实验配置"""
    experiment_id: str
    fault_type: FaultType
    target_component: str
    duration_seconds: int
    expected_recovery_time: int
    blast_radius: int  # 影响范围（如影响的知识晶体数）

class ResilienceEngine:
    """
    弹性引擎：混沌注入与反脆弱训练
    理念：杀不死我的使我更强大（What doesn't kill you makes you stronger）
    """
    
    def __init__(self, knowledge_system, db_path: str = "resilience/chaos.db"):
        self.knowledge = knowledge_system  # 关联的知识系统
        self.conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path)
        self._init_resilience_schema()
        
        self.is_chaos_mode = False
        self.injected_faults: List[Dict] = []
        self.recovery_hooks: Dict[FaultType, Callable] = {}
    
    def _init_resilience_schema(self):
        self.conn.executescript("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS chaos_experiments (
                experiment_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                start_time TIMESTAMP,
                end_time TIMESTAMP,
                fault_type TEXT,
                target_component TEXT,
                blast_radius INTEGER,
                detection_time_ms INTEGER,      -- 故障检测时间
                recovery_time_ms INTEGER,       -- 恢复时间
                data_loss_count INTEGER,        -- 数据丢失量
                lessons_learned TEXT            -- 生成的改进建议
            );
            
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS fault_vaccines (
                vaccine_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                fault_signature TEXT,           -- 故障特征（如SimHash）
                antibody_pattern TEXT,          -- 防御模式（JSON）
                effectiveness REAL,             -- 有效性（0-1）
                generated_from_experiment TEXT,
                created_at TIMESTAMP
            );
            
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS resilience_metrics (
                date TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                mttr_seconds REAL,              -- Mean Time To Recovery
                availability_percent REAL,
                anti_fragility_score REAL       -- 反脆弱评分：从故障中学习的速率
            );
        """)
        self.conn.commit()
    
    def inject_fault(self, fault_type: Optional[FaultType] = None, 
                    auto_recover: bool = True) -> ChaosExperiment:
        """
        注入故障：模拟系统灾难
        """
        if fault_type is None:
            fault_type = random.choice(list(FaultType))
        
        exp_id = f"CHAOS-{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d%H%M%S')}"
        
        # 根据故障类型执行具体破坏
        blast_radius = 0
        target = "unknown"
        
        if fault_type == FaultType.CRYSTAL_DELETE:
            target, blast_radius = self._delete_random_crystal()
        elif fault_type == FaultType.INDEX_CORRUPTION:
            target, blast_radius = self._corrupt_index()
        elif fault_type == FaultType.API_TIMEOUT:
            target = "kimi_api"
            blast_radius = 0  # 服务降级，不损坏数据
        
        experiment = ChaosExperiment(
            experiment_id=exp_id,
            fault_type=fault_type,
            target_component=target,
            duration_seconds=30,
            expected_recovery_time=60,
            blast_radius=blast_radius
        )
        
        # 记录实验
        self.conn.execute("""
            INSERT INTO chaos_experiments 
            (experiment_id, start_time, fault_type, target_component, blast_radius)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
        """, (exp_id, datetime.now(), fault_type.value, target, blast_radius))
        self.conn.commit()
        
        self.injected_faults.append({
            'exp_id': exp_id,
            'fault': fault_type,
            'start_time': time.time()
        })
        
        # 启动恢复计时（如果启用自动恢复）
        if auto_recover:
            threading.Thread(target=self._recovery_worker, 
                           args=(exp_id, fault_type, target)).start()
        
        return experiment
    
    def _delete_random_crystal(self) -> Tuple[str, int]:
        """删除随机知识晶体（带备份）"""
        # 先备份
        cursor = self.knowledge.conn.execute("""
            SELECT crystal_id FROM knowledge_crystals 
            WHERE status = 'active' ORDER BY RANDOM() LIMIT 1
        """)
        row = cursor.fetchone()
        
        if not row:
            return "none", 0
        
        crystal_id = row[0]
        
        # 备份到resilience表（而非真正删除，但标记为chaos_deleted）
        self.knowledge.conn.execute("""
            UPDATE knowledge_crystals 
            SET status = 'chaos_deleted', backup_tag = ?
            WHERE crystal_id = ?
        """, (f"backup-{datetime.now()}", crystal_id))
        self.knowledge.conn.commit()
        
        return f"crystal:{crystal_id}", 1
    
    def _corrupt_index(self) -> Tuple[str, int]:
        """损坏检索索引（SimHash位翻转）"""
        # 模拟：随机修改某些晶体的SimHash
        cursor = self.knowledge.conn.execute("""
            SELECT crystal_id, embedding_stub FROM knowledge_crystals LIMIT 5
        """)
        
        corrupted = 0
        for row in cursor.fetchall():
            cid, old_hash = row
            if old_hash:
                # 位翻转：随机翻转一个字符
                new_hash = old_hash[:5] + hex(random.randint(0,15))[2:] + old_hash[6:]
                self.knowledge.conn.execute("""
                    UPDATE knowledge_crystals SET embedding_stub = ? WHERE crystal_id = ?
                """, (new_hash, cid))
                corrupted += 1
        
        self.knowledge.conn.commit()
        return "simhash_index", corrupted
    
    def _recovery_worker(self, exp_id: str, fault_type: FaultType, target: str):
        """自动恢复工作线程"""
        start_recovery = time.time()
        
        # 模拟检测延迟
        time.sleep(random.uniform(1, 3))
        detection_time = int((time.time() - start_recovery) * 1000)
        
        # 执行恢复
        if fault_type == FaultType.CRYSTAL_DELETE:
            self._recover_deleted_crystal(target)
        elif fault_type == FaultType.INDEX_CORRUPTION:
            self._rebuild_index()
        
        recovery_time = int((time.time() - start_recovery) * 1000)
        
        # 记录结果
        self.conn.execute("""
            UPDATE chaos_experiments 
            SET detection_time_ms = ?, recovery_time_ms = ?, end_time = ?
            WHERE experiment_id = ?
        """, (detection_time, recovery_time, datetime.now(), exp_id))
        self.conn.commit()
        
        # 生成疫苗（从故障中学习）
        self._generate_vaccine(exp_id, fault_type, recovery_time)
    
    def _recover_deleted_crystal(self, target: str):
        """恢复被删除的晶体"""
        if target.startswith("crystal:"):
            cid = target.split(":")[1]
            self.knowledge.conn.execute("""
                UPDATE knowledge_crystals 
                SET status = 'active' 
                WHERE crystal_id = ? AND status = 'chaos_deleted'
            """, (cid,))
            self.knowledge.conn.commit()
    
    def _rebuild_index(self):
        """重建索引（简化：重新计算SimHash）"""
        cursor = self.knowledge.conn.execute("""
            SELECT crystal_id, answer FROM knowledge_crystals WHERE status = 'active'
        """)
        
        for row in cursor.fetchall():
            cid, content = row
            # 重新计算（简化）
            new_hash = hashlib.md5(content.encode()).hexdigest()[:16]
            self.knowledge.conn.execute("""
                UPDATE knowledge_crystals SET embedding_stub = ? WHERE crystal_id = ?
            """, (new_hash, cid))
        
        self.knowledge.conn.commit()
    
    def _generate_vaccine(self, exp_id: str, fault_type: FaultType, recovery_time: float):
        """
        从故障生成疫苗（反脆弱核心）
        如果恢复很快且数据无损，提取防御模式
        """
        # 生成故障签名（SimHash of fault characteristics）
        fault_sig = hashlib.md5(f"{fault_type.value}-{recovery_time}".encode()).hexdigest()[:16]
        
        # 如果恢复时间<预期，认为是有效防御
        is_effective = recovery_time < 60000  # <60秒
        
        antibody = {
            'detection_pattern': f"status='chaos_deleted'",
            'recovery_action': 'restore_from_backup',
            'redundancy_level': 2 if is_effective else 1
        }
        
        self.conn.execute("""
            INSERT OR REPLACE INTO fault_vaccines 
            (vaccine_id, fault_signature, antibody_pattern, effectiveness, 
             generated_from_experiment, created_at)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
        """, (f"VAC-{exp_id}", fault_sig, json.dumps(antibody), 
              1.0 if is_effective else 0.5, exp_id, datetime.now()))
        self.conn.commit()
    
    def get_resilience_report(self) -> Dict:
        """弹性报告：反脆弱评估"""
        # 计算MTTR（平均恢复时间）
        cursor = self.conn.execute("""
            SELECT AVG(recovery_time_ms) / 1000.0, 
                   AVG(detection_time_ms) / 1000.0,
                   COUNT(*)
            FROM chaos_experiments 
            WHERE end_time IS NOT NULL
        """)
        
        mttr, mtd, total = cursor.fetchone()
        
        # 反脆弱评分：从故障中学习的疫苗数 / 总故障数
        cursor = self.conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM fault_vaccines")
        vaccines = cursor.fetchone()[0]
        
        anti_fragility = vaccines / max(total, 1)
        
        return {
            'mean_time_to_recovery_seconds': mttr or 0,
            'mean_time_to_detection_seconds': mtd or 0,
            'total_chaos_experiments': total or 0,
            'active_vaccines': vaccines,
            'anti_fragility_score': anti_fragility,  # >0.5为反脆弱，<0.5为脆弱
            'status': 'anti-fragile' if anti_fragility > 0.5 else 'resilient' if anti_fragility > 0.2 else 'fragile'
        }
    
    def scheduled_chaos(self, interval_hours: int = 24):
        """定时混沌注入（类似疫苗接种，定期刺激免疫系统）"""
        # 实际应由外部调度器调用（如cron）
        # 此处仅记录建议
        self.conn.execute("""
            INSERT INTO resilience_metrics (date, mttr_seconds, availability_percent, anti_fragility_score)
            VALUES (date('now'), 0, 100, 0)
        """)
        self.conn.commit()

# 验证代码
def verify_resilience_engine():
    """验证弹性引擎"""
    import tempfile
    import shutil
    
    temp_dir = tempfile.mkdtemp()
    chaos_db = f"{temp_dir}/chaos.db"
    knowledge_db = f"{temp_dir}/knowledge.db"
    
    # 创建模拟知识库
    k_conn = sqlite3.connect(knowledge_db)
    k_conn.executescript("""
        CREATE TABLE knowledge_crystals (
            crystal_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
            answer TEXT,
            embedding_stub TEXT,
            status TEXT,
            backup_tag TEXT
        );
    """)
    for i in range(10):
        k_conn.execute("INSERT INTO knowledge_crystals VALUES (?, ?, ?, 'active', NULL)",
                      (f'crystal_{i}', f'Content {i}', hashlib.md5(f'{i}'.encode()).hexdigest()[:16]))
    k_conn.commit()
    
    class MockKnowledge:
        def __init__(self, db):
            self.conn = sqlite3.connect(db)
    
    try:
        mock_k = MockKnowledge(knowledge_db)
        engine = ResilienceEngine(mock_k, chaos_db)
        
        # 测试1：注入晶体删除故障
        exp = engine.inject_fault(FaultType.CRYSTAL_DELETE, auto_recover=False)
        assert exp.fault_type == FaultType.CRYSTAL_DELETE, "故障类型错误"
        
        # 检查是否标记删除
        k_conn = sqlite3.connect(knowledge_db)
        cursor = k_conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM knowledge_crystals WHERE status='chaos_deleted'")
        deleted = cursor.fetchone()[0]
        assert deleted > 0, "应至少有一个晶体被标记删除"
        k_conn.close()
        
        # 手动触发恢复
        engine._recovery_worker(exp.experiment_id, FaultType.CRYSTAL_DELETE, f"crystal:crystal_0")
        
        # 检查恢复
        time.sleep(0.5)  # 等待线程
        k_conn = sqlite3.connect(knowledge_db)
        cursor = k_conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM knowledge_crystals WHERE status='active'")
        active = cursor.fetchone()[0]
        k_conn.close()
        
        # 测试2：生成疫苗
        engine._generate_vaccine(exp.experiment_id, FaultType.CRYSTAL_DELETE, 500)  # 500ms快速恢复
        
        # 测试3：弹性报告
        report = engine.get_resilience_report()
        assert 'anti_fragility_score' in report, "缺少反脆弱评分"
        
        print("✅ 弹性引擎验证通过")
        print(f"   - 故障注入: {exp.fault_type.value} (范围:{exp.blast_radius})")
        print(f"   - 数据恢复: {active}个晶体恢复活跃")
        print(f"   - 疫苗生成: {report['active_vaccines']}个抗体")
        print(f"   - 反脆弱评分: {report['anti_fragility_score']:.2f} ({report['status']})")
        
        return True
        
    finally:
        shutil.rmtree(temp_dir, ignore_errors=True)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    verify_resilience_engine()
