## 第二十四轮洞察：超图与高阶关系（Hypergraph）——超越二元关系的认知

### 第一性原理洞察

现有知识图谱局限于二元关系（A→B），但真实认知多为多元关系（A,B,C在上下文D中共同导出E）。超图（Hypergraph）中一条边可连接任意数量节点，能表达高阶交互（Emergent Properties）。

### 方法论：Hypergraph Neural Network (HGN) 本地实现

建立高阶邻接（High-order Adjacency）： - 超边（Hyperedges）：连接2+个实体的语义单元（如”五路共识”同时连接confucius/guanyin/simon/blue/satisfactory） - 星形展开（Star Expansion）：超图→二分图（节点-超边），保持可计算性 - 高阶消息传递：知识更新通过超边传播，一次性影响多个关联节点

### 技术选型理由

为何用星形展开而非直接超图：避免重新实现图算法，利用现有Kùzu的二分图能力，通过引入”超边节点”间接实现。

为何保留关系型存储：超边的属性（创建时间、置信度）仍用SQLite存储，星形结构仅用于遍历。

### 代码级实施方案

# hypergraph/hyper_knowledge_graph.py
# 超图知识系统：支持N元高阶关系

import sqlite3
import json
import hashlib
from typing import Dict, List, Set, Tuple, Optional, FrozenSet
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from collections import defaultdict

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class HyperEdge:
    """超边：连接多个节点的N元关系"""
    edge_id: str
    relation_type: str           # 如"五路共识"、"三角验证"、"多元决策"
    node_set: FrozenSet[str]     # 连接的节点集合（无序，体现高阶性）
    hyper_attributes: Dict       # 超边独有属性（非节点属性）
    order: int                   # 阶数（|node_set|）

class HyperKnowledgeGraph:
    """
    超图知识系统：支持任意元关系
    通过星形展开（Star Expansion）实现：添加超边节点，连接所有成员节点
    """
    
    def __init__(self, base_graph_db: str, db_path: str = "hypergraph/hyper.db"):
        self.base_db = base_graph_db  # 底层Kùzu/SQLite图
        self.conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path)
        self._init_hyper_schema()
        self._hyper_node_counter = 0
    
    def _init_hyper_schema(self):
        """超图Schema（星形展开存储）"""
        self.conn.executescript("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS hyper_edges (
                edge_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                relation_type TEXT,
                node_set_hash TEXT,       -- 节点集合的哈希（去重索引）
                node_count INTEGER,       -- 阶数N
                node_members TEXT,        -- JSON数组（有序存储，但逻辑无序）
                hyper_attributes TEXT,    -- JSON
                created_at TIMESTAMP,
                weight REAL DEFAULT 1.0   -- 超边权重（高阶关系强度）
            );
            
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS star_expansion (
                hyper_edge_id TEXT,
                member_node TEXT,
                edge_role TEXT,           -- 在超边中的角色（如"提议者"、"验证者"）
                PRIMARY KEY (hyper_edge_id, member_node)
            );
            
            CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_hyper_hash ON hyper_edges(node_set_hash);
            CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_star_member ON star_expansion(member_node);
            
            -- 高阶邻接表（缓存）
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS high_order_adjacency (
                node_a TEXT,
                node_b TEXT,
                common_hyper_edges TEXT,  -- JSON数组，共享的超边ID
                high_order_distance INTEGER,  -- 高阶距离（通过超边连接的最短路径）
                PRIMARY KEY (node_a, node_b)
            );
        """)
        self.conn.commit()
    
    def create_hyper_edge(self, nodes: List[str], relation_type: str, 
                         attributes: Optional[Dict] = None,
                         roles: Optional[List[str]] = None) -> HyperEdge:
        """
        创建N元超边（N=len(nodes)）
        例如：['confucius', 'guanyin', 'simon', 'blue', 'satisfactory'] + "五路共识"
        """
        if len(nodes) < 2:
            raise ValueError("Hyperedge must connect at least 2 nodes")
        
        # 生成唯一ID（基于内容哈希，确保幂等）
        content = f"{relation_type}|{sorted(nodes)}|{json.dumps(attributes, sort_keys=True)}"
        edge_id = f"HYPER-{hashlib.sha256(content.encode()).hexdigest()[:12]}"
        
        node_set = frozenset(nodes)
        node_set_hash = hashlib.sha256(str(sorted(nodes)).encode()).hexdigest()[:16]
        
        # 存储超边
        self.conn.execute("""
            INSERT OR REPLACE INTO hyper_edges 
            (edge_id, relation_type, node_set_hash, node_count, node_members, 
             hyper_attributes, created_at, weight)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, datetime('now'), 1.0)
        """, (edge_id, relation_type, node_set_hash, len(nodes), 
              json.dumps(nodes), json.dumps(attributes or {})))
        
        # 星形展开：超边作为虚拟节点，连接所有成员
        for i, node in enumerate(nodes):
            role = roles[i] if roles and i < len(roles) else f"member_{i}"
            self.conn.execute("""
                INSERT OR REPLACE INTO star_expansion 
                (hyper_edge_id, member_node, edge_role)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?)
            """, (edge_id, node, role))
        
        self.conn.commit()
        
        # 更新高阶邻接表（异步或延迟计算，此处简化立即计算）
        self._update_high_order_adjacency(edge_id, nodes)
        
        return HyperEdge(
            edge_id=edge_id,
            relation_type=relation_type,
            node_set=node_set,
            hyper_attributes=attributes or {},
            order=len(nodes)
        )
    
    def _update_high_order_adjacency(self, edge_id: str, nodes: List[str]):
        """更新高阶邻接表：超边内所有节点对的高阶距离=1"""
        for i, node_a in enumerate(nodes):
            for node_b in nodes[i+1:]:
                # 查询现有共享超边
                cursor = self.conn.execute("""
                    SELECT common_hyper_edges FROM high_order_adjacency
                    WHERE node_a = ? AND node_b = ?
                """, (min(node_a, node_b), max(node_a, node_b)))
                
                row = cursor.fetchone()
                if row:
                    existing = json.loads(row[0])
                    if edge_id not in existing:
                        existing.append(edge_id)
                        self.conn.execute("""
                            UPDATE high_order_adjacency 
                            SET common_hyper_edges = ?, high_order_distance = 1
                            WHERE node_a = ? AND node_b = ?
                        """, (json.dumps(existing), min(node_a, node_b), max(node_a, node_b)))
                else:
                    self.conn.execute("""
                        INSERT INTO high_order_adjacency 
                        (node_a, node_b, common_hyper_edges, high_order_distance)
                        VALUES (?, ?, ?, 1)
                    """, (min(node_a, node_b), max(node_a, node_b), json.dumps([edge_id])))
        
        self.conn.commit()
    
    def hyper_neighborhood(self, node: str, order: int = 1) -> Dict[str, List[Dict]]:
        """
        高阶邻域查询：通过超边连接的邻居（不同于图的直接邻居）
        例如：查询与"confucius"共同参与的所有五路共识超边
        """
        # 查找包含该节点的所有超边
        cursor = self.conn.execute("""
            SELECT h.edge_id, h.relation_type, h.node_members, h.hyper_attributes, s.edge_role
            FROM hyper_edges h
            JOIN star_expansion s ON h.edge_id = s.hyper_edge_id
            WHERE s.member_node = ?
        """, (node,))
        
        hyper_memberships = []
        for row in cursor.fetchall():
            edge_id, rel_type, members, attrs, role = row
            other_nodes = [n for n in json.loads(members) if n != node]
            hyper_memberships.append({
                'hyper_edge_id': edge_id,
                'relation_type': rel_type,
                'my_role': role,
                'co_members': other_nodes,
                'order': len(json.loads(members)),
                'attributes': json.loads(attrs)
            })
        
        # 按关系类型分组
        by_type = defaultdict(list)
        for membership in hyper_memberships:
            by_type[membership['relation_type']].append(membership)
        
        return dict(by_type)
    
    def high_order_message_passing(self, initial_nodes: List[str], 
                                    iterations: int = 2) -> Dict[str, float]:
        """
        高阶消息传递：通过超边的集体更新
        与图神经网络不同，消息通过超边同时传递给所有成员（广播语义）
        """
        # 初始化节点状态
        node_states = {node: 1.0 for node in initial_nodes}
        
        for _ in range(iterations):
            new_states = defaultdict(float)
            
            # 查找涉及当前活跃节点的所有超边
            active_nodes = list(node_states.keys())
            placeholders = ','.join('?' * len(active_nodes))
            
            cursor = self.conn.execute(f"""
                SELECT DISTINCT h.edge_id, h.node_members, h.weight
                FROM hyper_edges h
                JOIN star_expansion s ON h.edge_id = s.hyper_edge_id
                WHERE s.member_node IN ({placeholders})
            """, active_nodes)
            
            for row in cursor.fetchall():
                edge_id, members_json, weight = row
                members = json.loads(members_json)
                
                # 高阶聚合：超边内所有节点的状态平均
                member_states = [node_states.get(m, 0.0) for m in members]
                hyper_message = np.mean(member_states) * weight
                
                # 广播给所有成员（高阶特性：一次影响多个）
                for member in members:
                    new_states[member] += hyper_message / len(members)  # 分配权重
            
            # 更新状态
            for node, val in new_states.items():
                node_states[node] = val
        
        return dict(node_states)
    
    def detect_high_order_cliques(self, min_order: int = 3) -> List[Dict]:
        """
        检测高阶团（N-clique）：N个节点共同出现在多个超边中
        表示深度协作关系（如五路图腾长期稳定合作）
        """
        # 查找频繁共同出现的节点对
        cursor = self.conn.execute("""
            SELECT node_a, node_b, common_hyper_edges
            FROM high_order_adjacency
            WHERE json_array_length(common_hyper_edges) >= 2
        """)
        
        # 构建共现图（基于高阶邻接）
        cooccurrence_graph = defaultdict(set)
        for row in cursor.fetchall():
            node_a, node_b, edges_json = row
            cooccurrence_graph[node_a].add(node_b)
            cooccurrence_graph[node_b].add(node_a)
        
        # 查找团（简化：查找三角形/四边形）
        cliques = []
        nodes = list(cooccurrence_graph.keys())
        
        for i, a in enumerate(nodes):
            for b in cooccurrence_graph[a]:
                if b <= a:
                    continue
                for c in cooccurrence_graph[b]:
                    if c <= b or c not in cooccurrence_graph[a]:
                        continue
                    # 找到三角形abc
                    # 检查是否有共同的高阶超边包含这三者
                    common_edges = self._get_common_hyper_edges([a, b, c])
                    if len(common_edges) >= min_order:
                        cliques.append({
                            'nodes': [a, b, c],
                            'order': 3,
                            'common_hyper_edges': common_edges,
                            'clique_strength': len(common_edges)
                        })
        
        return sorted(cliques, key=lambda x: x['clique_strength'], reverse=True)
    
    def _get_common_hyper_edges(self, node_set: List[str]) -> List[str]:
        """获取包含所有指定节点的超边"""
        if not node_set:
            return []
        
        # 查找包含第一个节点的超边
        cursor = self.conn.execute("""
            SELECT hyper_edge_id FROM star_expansion WHERE member_node = ?
        """, (node_set[0],))
        
        candidate_edges = [row[0] for row in cursor.fetchall()]
        
        # 筛选包含所有其他节点的超边
        common = []
        for edge_id in candidate_edges:
            cursor = self.conn.execute("""
                SELECT COUNT(*) FROM star_expansion 
                WHERE hyper_edge_id = ? AND member_node IN ({})
            """.format(','.join('?' * len(node_set))), [edge_id] + node_set[1:])
            
            count = cursor.fetchone()[0]
            if count == len(node_set) - 1:  # 包含所有其他节点
                common.append(edge_id)
        
        return common
    
    def get_hypergraph_stats(self) -> Dict:
        """超图统计"""
        cursor = self.conn.execute("""
            SELECT order, COUNT(*) FROM hyper_edges GROUP BY order
        """)
        order_dist = {row[0]: row[1] for row in cursor.fetchall()}
        
        cursor = self.conn.execute("""
            SELECT AVG(node_count) FROM hyper_edges
        """)
        avg_order = cursor.fetchone()[0] or 0
        
        return {
            'total_hyper_edges': sum(order_dist.values()),
            'order_distribution': order_dist,
            'average_order': avg_order,
            'max_order': max(order_dist.keys()) if order_dist else 0,
            'star_expansion_ratio': self._calculate_expansion_ratio()
        }
    
    def _calculate_expansion_ratio(self) -> float:
        """计算星形展开膨胀率（超图 vs 普通图的边数比）"""
        cursor = self.conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM star_expansion")
        star_edges = cursor.fetchone()[0]
        
        # 如果展开为普通图，需要N(N-1)/2条边（完全图）
        cursor = self.conn.execute("""
            SELECT SUM(node_count * (node_count - 1) / 2) FROM hyper_edges
        """)
        potential_edges = cursor.fetchone()[0] or 1
        
        return star_edges / potential_edges if potential_edges > 0 else 0

# 验证代码
def verify_hypergraph():
    """验证超图系统"""
    import tempfile
    import shutil
    
    temp_dir = tempfile.mkdtemp()
    db_path = f"{temp_dir}/hyper.db"
    
    try:
        hkg = HyperKnowledgeGraph("base.db", db_path)
        
        # 测试1：创建五元超边（五路共识）
        five_way = hkg.create_hyper_edge(
            nodes=['confucius', 'guanyin', 'simon', 'blue_army', 'satisfactory'],
            relation_type='五路共识',
            attributes={'decision': '通过', 'confidence': 0.95},
            roles=['伦理审查', '慈悲关怀', '理性边界', '风险审计', '最终决策']
        )
        
        assert five_way.order == 5, f"超边阶数错误: {five_way.order}"
        print(f"   创建五元超边: {five_way.edge_id}")
        
        # 测试2：高阶邻域查询
        neighborhood = hkg.hyper_neighborhood('confucius')
        assert '五路共识' in neighborhood, "邻域查询失败"
        print(f"   高阶邻域: 发现{len(neighborhood.get('五路共识', []))}个五路共识关系")
        
        # 测试3：高阶消息传递
        states = hkg.high_order_message_passing(['confucius', 'guanyin'], iterations=2)
        assert 'simon' in states, "消息未传播到超边其他成员"
        print(f"   高阶消息传递: 影响{len(states)}个节点")
        
        # 测试4：高阶团检测
        # 创建更多三角关系
        hkg.create_hyper_edge(['confucius', 'guanyin', 'blue_army'], '伦理审计三角')
        hkg.create_hyper_edge(['confucius', 'guanyin', 'blue_army'], '风险伦理联合')
        
        cliques = hkg.detect_high_order_cliques(min_order=2)
        assert len(cliques) > 0, "未检测到高阶团"
        print(f"   高阶团检测: 发现{len(cliques)}个三角团")
        
        # 测试5：统计
        stats = hkg.get_hypergraph_stats()
        assert stats['average_order'] > 2, "平均阶数过低"
        print(f"   超图统计: 平均阶数={stats['average_order']:.1f}, 最大阶数={stats['max_order']}")
        
        print("✅ 超图知识系统验证通过")
        return True
        
    finally:
        shutil.rmtree(temp_dir, ignore_errors=True)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    verify_hypergraph()

## 第二十五轮洞察：多世界诠释（Many-Worlds）——知识的平行历史

### 第一性原理洞察

量子力学的多世界诠释：每次量子测量宇宙分裂为多个平行世界。知识系统同理：每个决策点（如”是否采用HyperCompressor”）都分裂出平行知识宇宙，系统需能跨世界比较最优路径。

### 方法论：Many-Worlds Knowledge Interpretation (MWKI)

建立世界线（World Lines）： - 分支事件（Branching Events）：决策点导致世界分裂（类似Git分支但自动演化） - 世界线密度（World-line Density）：不同历史路径的概率幅 - 跨世界干涉（Cross-world Interference）：相似世界间的知识共振（如两个成功世界线的经验叠加）

### 技术选型理由

为何用版本控制模拟世界线：Git的分支模型天然适合多世界，但需扩展为自动演化（各世界独立时间流逝）。

为何世界间可干涉：类似量子隧穿，相似决策路径的知识可”泄漏”到相邻世界（经验迁移）。

### 代码级实施方案

# many_worlds/world_line_manager.py
# 多世界诠释：知识的平行历史与跨世界比较

import sqlite3
import json
import random
import hashlib
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Set, Tuple
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from enum import Enum

class WorldStatus(Enum):
    ACTIVE = "active"          # 正在演化
    PRUNED = "pruned"          # 被裁剪（概率过低）
    MERGED = "merged"          # 与其他世界合并
    BASELINE = "baseline"      # 基线世界（主时间线）

@dataclass
class WorldLine:
    """世界线：独立演化的知识历史"""
    world_id: str              # 世界唯一标识（如"W-2026-001"）
    parent_world: Optional[str]  # 父世界（分支来源）
    branch_event: str            # 分支事件描述
    creation_time: datetime
    status: WorldStatus
    probability_amplitude: complex  # 概率幅（模平方=存在概率）
    knowledge_state: Dict          # 该世界的知识快照
    divergence_meter: float        # 与基线的差异度（0-1）

class ManyWorldsKnowledge:
    """
    多世界知识系统：管理平行知识历史
    核心：每个重大决策创建分支世界，自动演化，定期修剪
    """
    
    def __init__(self, db_path: str = "many_worlds/mw.db"):
        self.conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path)
        self._init_worlds_schema()
        self.baseline_world = "W-BASELINE"
        self._ensure_baseline_exists()
    
    def _init_worlds_schema(self):
        self.conn.executescript("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS world_lines (
                world_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                parent_world TEXT,
                branch_event TEXT,
                creation_time TIMESTAMP,
                status TEXT,
                prob_real REAL,          -- 概率幅实部
                prob_imag REAL,          -- 概率幅虚部
                knowledge_state TEXT,    -- JSON
                divergence_meter REAL,
                last_evolution TIMESTAMP
            );
            
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS world_interference (
                world_a TEXT,
                world_b TEXT,
                interference_strength REAL,  -- 世界间干涉强度（相似度）
                knowledge_leakage TEXT,      -- JSON，知识迁移记录
                last_coupling TIMESTAMP,
                PRIMARY KEY (world_a, world_b)
            );
            
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS cross_world_queries (
                query_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                target_worlds TEXT,      -- JSON数组，查询的世界范围
                aggregation_method TEXT, -- 'probability_weighted', 'best_case', 'worst_case'
                result TEXT,
                timestamp TIMESTAMP
            );
            
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS pruning_log (
                prune_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                pruned_world TEXT,
                reason TEXT,             -- 'low_probability', 'convergence', 'manual'
                merged_into TEXT,        -- 合并到哪个世界（如果是收敛）
                pruned_at TIMESTAMP
            );
        """)
        self.conn.commit()
    
    def _ensure_baseline_exists(self):
        """确保基线世界存在"""
        self.conn.execute("""
            INSERT OR IGNORE INTO world_lines 
            (world_id, parent_world, branch_event, creation_time, status, 
             prob_real, prob_imag, divergence_meter)
            VALUES (?, NULL, 'origin', datetime('now'), 'baseline', 1.0, 0.0, 0.0)
        """, (self.baseline_world,))
        self.conn.commit()
    
    def branch_world(self, parent_world: str, event: str, 
                     initial_probability: float = 0.5) -> WorldLine:
        """
        世界分裂：在父世界基础上创建分支世界
        例如："决定采用HyperCompressor" vs "决定采用全量Embedding"
        """
        # 生成世界ID（基于父世界+事件哈希）
        world_id = f"W-{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d%H%M%S')}-{hashlib.sha256(event.encode()).hexdigest()[:4]}"
        
        # 获取父世界状态
        cursor = self.conn.execute("""
            SELECT knowledge_state FROM world_lines WHERE world_id = ?
        """, (parent_world,))
        
        row = cursor.fetchone()
        parent_state = json.loads(row[0]) if row and row[0] else {}
        
        # 新世界的概率幅（与父世界共享概率质量）
        # 简化：平分概率（实际应基于决策不确定性）
        parent_prob = self._get_world_probability(parent_world)
        new_prob = parent_prob * initial_probability
        self._update_world_probability(parent_world, parent_prob * (1 - initial_probability))
        
        # 创建世界
        world = WorldLine(
            world_id=world_id,
            parent_world=parent_world,
            branch_event=event,
            creation_time=datetime.now(),
            status=WorldStatus.ACTIVE,
            probability_amplitude=complex(np.sqrt(new_prob), 0),
            knowledge_state=parent_state.copy(),  # 继承父状态
            divergence_meter=0.01  # 初始差异很小
        )
        
        self.conn.execute("""
            INSERT INTO world_lines 
            (world_id, parent_world, branch_event, creation_time, status,
             prob_real, prob_imag, knowledge_state, divergence_meter, last_evolution)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, datetime('now'))
        """, (world_id, parent_world, event, world.creation_time, world.status.value,
              world.probability_amplitude.real, world.probability_amplitude.imag,
              json.dumps(world.knowledge_state), world.divergence_meter))
        
        self.conn.commit()
        return world
    
    def _get_world_probability(self, world_id: str) -> float:
        """获取世界存在概率（概率幅模平方）"""
        cursor = self.conn.execute("""
            SELECT prob_real, prob_imag FROM world_lines WHERE world_id = ?
        """, (world_id,))
        
        row = cursor.fetchone()
        if not row:
            return 0.0
        
        amp = complex(row[0], row[1])
        return abs(amp)**2
    
    def _update_world_probability(self, world_id: str, new_prob: float):
        """更新世界概率"""
        # 保持相位，调整模
        cursor = self.conn.execute("""
            SELECT prob_real, prob_imag FROM world_lines WHERE world_id = ?
        """, (world_id,))
        
        row = cursor.fetchone()
        if row:
            old_amp = complex(row[0], row[1])
            phase = np.angle(old_amp) if abs(old_amp) > 0 else 0
            new_amp = np.sqrt(new_prob) * np.exp(1j * phase)
            
            self.conn.execute("""
                UPDATE world_lines SET prob_real = ?, prob_imag = ? WHERE world_id = ?
            """, (new_amp.real, new_amp.imag, world_id))
            self.conn.commit()
    
    def evolve_world(self, world_id: str, time_steps: int = 1):
        """
        世界演化：独立时间流逝，知识状态更新
        不同世界可能有不同的物理常数（如token价格、API可用性）
        """
        cursor = self.conn.execute("""
            SELECT knowledge_state, divergence_meter FROM world_lines WHERE world_id = ?
        """, (world_id,))
        
        row = cursor.fetchone()
        if not row:
            return
        
        state = json.loads(row[0])
        divergence = row[1]
        
        # 模拟演化（简化：随机漂移+特定世界规则）
        # 例如：在"HyperCompressor世界"，Token成本指数下降
        # 在"全量Embedding世界"，成本线性增长
        
        if "采用HyperCompressor" in str(state):
            state['token_cost_trend'] = state.get('token_cost_trend', 1.0) * 0.9
        else:
            state['token_cost_trend'] = state.get('token_cost_trend', 1.0) * 1.1
        
        divergence = min(divergence + 0.05, 1.0)  # 随时间差异增大
        
        self.conn.execute("""
            UPDATE world_lines 
            SET knowledge_state = ?, divergence_meter = ?, last_evolution = datetime('now')
            WHERE world_id = ?
        """, (json.dumps(state), divergence, world_id))
        self.conn.commit()
    
    def calculate_world_interference(self, world_a: str, world_b: str) -> float:
        """
        计算世界间干涉强度（类似量子干涉）
        相似世界（高重叠知识）产生强干涉（知识可迁移）
        """
        # 获取两世界的知识状态
        cursor = self.conn.execute("""
            SELECT knowledge_state FROM world_lines WHERE world_id IN (?, ?)
        """, (world_a, world_b))
        
        rows = cursor.fetchall()
        if len(rows) < 2:
            return 0.0
        
        state_a = json.loads(rows[0][0]) if rows[0][0] else {}
        state_b = json.loads(rows[1][0]) if rows[1][0] else {}
        
        # 计算知识重叠（Jaccard相似度）
        keys_a = set(state_a.keys())
        keys_b = set(state_b.keys())
        
        if not keys_a or not keys_b:
            return 0.0
        
        intersection = len(keys_a & keys_b)
        union = len(keys_a | keys_b)
        similarity = intersection / union if union > 0 else 0
        
        # 干涉强度 = 相似度 * 概率幅乘积（简化）
        prob_a = self._get_world_probability(world_a)
        prob_b = self._get_world_probability(world_b)
        
        interference = similarity * np.sqrt(prob_a * prob_b)
        
        # 存储干涉关系
        self.conn.execute("""
            INSERT OR REPLACE INTO world_interference 
            (world_a, world_b, interference_strength, last_coupling)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, datetime('now'))
        """, (min(world_a, world_b), max(world_a, world_b), interference))
        self.conn.commit()
        
        return interference
    
    def cross_world_leakage(self, source_world: str, target_world: str, 
                           knowledge_key: str):
        """
        跨世界知识泄漏：成功世界的经验自动扩散到相似世界
        类似量子隧穿，但单向（从高效世界到低效世界）
        """
        # 检查干涉强度
        interference = self.calculate_world_interference(source_world, target_world)
        
        if interference < 0.3:
            return {'success': False, 'reason': 'Interference too weak'}
        
        # 获取源世界知识
        cursor = self.conn.execute("""
            SELECT knowledge_state FROM world_lines WHERE world_id = ?
        """, (source_world,))
        
        row = cursor.fetchone()
        if not row:
            return {'success': False, 'reason': 'Source world not found'}
        
        source_state = json.loads(row[0])
        if knowledge_key not in source_state:
            return {'success': False, reason: 'Knowledge not in source'}
        
        # 泄漏到目标世界（部分复制，有损耗）
        leaked_value = source_state[knowledge_key]
        
        cursor = self.conn.execute("""
            SELECT knowledge_state FROM world_lines WHERE world_id = ?
        """, (target_world,))
        
        row = cursor.fetchone()
        if not row:
            return {'success': False, 'reason': 'Target world not found'}
        
        target_state = json.loads(row[0])
        target_state[f"leaked_from_{source_world}_{knowledge_key}"] = leaked_value
        
        self.conn.execute("""
            UPDATE world_lines SET knowledge_state = ? WHERE world_id = ?
        """, (json.dumps(target_state), target_world))
        
        # 记录泄漏
        self.conn.execute("""
            UPDATE world_interference 
            SET knowledge_leakage = json_insert(COALESCE(knowledge_leakage, '{}'), 
                '$.recent', json_object('key', ?, 'time', datetime('now')))
            WHERE (world_a = ? AND world_b = ?) OR (world_a = ? AND world_b = ?)
        """, (knowledge_key, min(source_world, target_world), max(source_world, target_world),
              min(source_world, target_world), max(source_world, target_world)))
        
        self.conn.commit()
        
        return {
            'success': True,
            'leakage_fidelity': interference,  # 保真度=干涉强度
            'knowledge_transferred': knowledge_key
        }
    
    def prune_worlds(self, threshold: float = 0.01):
        """
        世界修剪：移除概率过低的世界（退相干）
        类似量子退相干，低概率世界不再可观测
        """
        cursor = self.conn.execute("""
            SELECT world_id, prob_real, prob_imag FROM world_lines 
            WHERE status = 'active' AND world_id != ?
        """, (self.baseline_world,))
        
        pruned = []
        for row in cursor.fetchall():
            world_id, real, imag = row
            prob = abs(complex(real, imag))**2
            
            if prob < threshold:
                # 修剪：合并到父世界或标记为pruned
                self.conn.execute("""
                    UPDATE world_lines SET status = 'pruned' WHERE world_id = ?
                """, (world_id,))
                
                self.conn.execute("""
                    INSERT INTO pruning_log (pruned_world, reason, pruned_at)
                    VALUES (?, 'low_probability', datetime('now'))
                """, (world_id,))
                
                pruned.append(world_id)
        
        self.conn.commit()
        return pruned
    
    def cross_world_comparison(self, query: str, 
                               target_worlds: Optional[List[str]] = None) -> Dict:
        """
        跨世界查询：比较不同世界对同一问题的答案
        例如："在这个世界用方案A，在平行世界用方案B，结果如何？"
        """
        if target_worlds is None:
            # 查询所有活跃世界
            cursor = self.conn.execute("""
                SELECT world_id FROM world_lines WHERE status = 'active'
            """)
            target_worlds = [row[0] for row in cursor.fetchall()]
        
        results = {}
        for world_id in target_worlds:
            # 模拟在该世界的查询结果（基于知识状态）
            cursor = self.conn.execute("""
                SELECT knowledge_state FROM world_lines WHERE world_id = ?
            """, (world_id,))
            
            row = cursor.fetchone()
            if row:
                state = json.loads(row[0])
                # 简化：查找知识状态中匹配query的键
                matching = {k: v for k, v in state.items() if query.lower() in k.lower()}
                results[world_id] = matching
        
        # 概率加权聚合
        weighted_result = {}
        for world_id, data in results.items():
            prob = self._get_world_probability(world_id)
            for k, v in data.items():
                if k not in weighted_result:
                    weighted_result[k] = {'values': [], 'total_prob': 0}
                weighted_result[k]['values'].append((v, prob))
                weighted_result[k]['total_prob'] += prob
        
        # 归一化
        for k in weighted_result:
            if weighted_result[k]['total_prob'] > 0:
                weighted_avg = sum(v * p for v, p in weighted_result[k]['values']) / weighted_result[k]['total_prob']
                weighted_result[k]['expected_value'] = weighted_avg
        
        return {
            'query': query,
            'worlds_consulted': len(target_worlds),
            'probability_weighted_result': weighted_result,
            'best_case_world': max(results.items(), key=lambda x: len(x[1]))[0] if results else None,
            'parallel_realities_count': len(target_worlds)
        }

# 验证代码
def verify_many_worlds():
    """验证多世界系统"""
    import tempfile
    import shutil
    
    temp_dir = tempfile.mkdtemp()
    db_path = f"{temp_dir}/mw.db"
    
    try:
        mw = ManyWorldsKnowledge(db_path)
        
        # 测试1：创建分支世界
        world_a = mw.branch_world(mw.baseline_world, "决定采用HyperCompressor", initial_probability=0.6)
        world_b = mw.branch_world(mw.baseline_world, "决定采用全量Embedding", initial_probability=0.4)
        
        assert world_a.status == WorldStatus.ACTIVE, "世界创建失败"
        print(f"   分支世界A: {world_a.world_id} (概率{mw._get_world_probability(world_a.world_id):.1%})")
        print(f"   分支世界B: {world_b.world_id} (概率{mw._get_world_probability(world_b.world_id):.1%})")
        
        # 测试2：世界演化（不同物理常数）
        for _ in range(3):
            mw.evolve_world(world_a.world_id)
            mw.evolve_world(world_b.world_id)
        
        # 检查差异度
        cursor = mw.conn.execute("SELECT divergence_meter FROM world_lines WHERE world_id=?", (world_a.world_id,))
        div_a = cursor.fetchone()[0]
        print(f"   世界A差异度: {div_a:.2f}")
        
        # 测试3：世界干涉
        interference = mw.calculate_world_interference(world_a.world_id, world_b.world_id)
        print(f"   世界间干涉强度: {interference:.2f}")
        
        # 测试4：知识泄漏（从高效世界到低效世界）
        # 假设世界A更高效（HyperCompressor），知识泄漏到世界B
        leak_result = mw.cross_world_leakage(world_a.world_id, world_b.world_id, 'token_cost_trend')
        print(f"   知识泄漏: {'成功' if leak_result['success'] else '失败'} (保真度{leak_result.get('leakage_fidelity', 0):.2f})")
        
        # 测试5：跨世界查询
        comparison = mw.cross_world_comparison("token_cost")
        assert 'probability_weighted_result' in comparison, "跨世界查询失败"
        print(f"   跨世界查询: 咨询了{comparison['parallel_realities_count']}个平行现实")
        
        # 测试6：世界修剪
        pruned = mw.prune_worlds(threshold=0.05)  # 修剪概率<5%的世界
        print(f"   世界修剪: 移除了{len(pruned)}个低概率世界")
        
        print("✅ 多世界诠释系统验证通过")
        return True
        
    finally:
        shutil.rmtree(temp_dir, ignore_errors=True)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    verify_many_worlds()

## 第二十六轮洞察：递归自指与自修改（Recursive Self-Reference）——哥德尔式自我完善

### 第一性原理洞察

哥德尔不完备定理表明，足够复杂的系统无法自证完备，但可以引用和修改自身。系统应能： 1. 读取自身源代码：分析当前架构的局限性 2. 生成改进补丁：基于性能瓶颈自动提出优化 3. 热更新执行：在运行中替换自身模块（类似生物新陈代谢）

### 方法论：Quine-based Self-Modification

建立自修改循环： - Quine程序：能打印自身源代码的程序（自我描述） - 反射层（Reflective Tower）：系统在不同元层级观察自身 - 生成式编程：代码即数据，通过模板生成新代码并执行

### 技术选型理由

为何用AST而非字符串操作：Python的ast模块确保语法正确性，避免自修改导致语法错误。

为何分层修改（渐进式）：避免”连续自修改导致失控”，每次修改需经第17轮的五路共识验证。

### 代码级实施方案

# self_reference/reflexive_system.py
# 递归自指系统：能分析、修改、优化自身源代码

import ast
import inspect
import textwrap
import hashlib
import sqlite3
import types
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Callable, Any
from dataclasses import dataclass
import importlib.util
import sys

@dataclass
class CodePatch:
    """代码补丁"""
    target_module: str
    target_function: str
    patch_type: str          # 'optimization', 'bugfix', 'feature'
    original_hash: str       # 原代码哈希（确保修改正确目标）
    patched_code: str          # 新代码
    validation_tests: List[str]  # 验证测试用例
    meta_level: int          # 元层级（0=对象层，1=元层，2=元元层...）

class ReflexiveSelfModifier:
    """
    递归自指修改器：系统能读取、分析、修改自身
    终极自主性：自我完善、自我修复、自我进化
    """
    
    def __init__(self, db_path: str = "self_reference/reflex.db"):
        self.conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path)
        self._init_reflex_schema()
        self.meta_level = 0  # 当前运行层级
        self.self_hash = self._compute_self_hash()
    
    def _init_reflex_schema(self):
        self.conn.executescript("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS self_snapshots (
                snapshot_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                timestamp TIMESTAMP,
                system_hash TEXT,
                total_modules INTEGER,
                total_functions INTEGER,
                complexity_score REAL,
                source_code_archive TEXT  -- BLOB或路径
            );
            
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS generated_patches (
                patch_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                snapshot_id TEXT,
                target_file TEXT,
                target_function TEXT,
                patch_type TEXT,
                original_code_hash TEXT,
                patch_diff TEXT,          -- unified diff格式
                generation_reason TEXT,   -- 为什么生成此补丁
                applied BOOLEAN DEFAULT 0,
                application_time TIMESTAMP,
                validation_result TEXT    -- 'pass', 'fail', 'pending'
            );
            
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS meta_stack (
                level INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                observer_module TEXT,     -- 哪个模块在观察下层
                observation_target TEXT,  -- 观察目标
                reflection_depth INTEGER  -- 反射深度
            );
        """)
        self.conn.commit()
    
    def _compute_self_hash(self) -> str:
        """计算当前系统状态的哈希（自我识别）"""
        # 读取自身模块文件（简化：仅计算当前文件哈希）
        try:
            with open(__file__, 'rb') as f:
                return hashlib.sha256(f.read()).hexdigest()[:16]
        except:
            return "unknown"
    
    def introspect_module(self, module_name: str) -> Dict:
        """
        内省模块：分析自身结构（类似生物的自我感知）
        """
        try:
            spec = importlib.util.find_spec(module_name)
            if not spec or not spec.origin:
                return {'error': 'Module not found'}
            
            with open(spec.origin, 'r') as f:
                source = f.read()
            
            # AST解析
            tree = ast.parse(source)
            
            functions = []
            classes = []
            imports = []
            
            for node in ast.walk(tree):
                if isinstance(node, ast.FunctionDef):
                    functions.append({
                        'name': node.name,
                        'args': len(node.args.args),
                        'complexity': len(list(ast.walk(node))),
                        'line': node.lineno
                    })
                elif isinstance(node, ast.ClassDef):
                    classes.append({
                        'name': node.name,
                        'methods': len([n for n in node.body if isinstance(n, ast.FunctionDef)]),
                        'line': node.lineno
                    })
                elif isinstance(node, (ast.Import, ast.ImportFrom)):
                    imports.append(ast.unparse(node))
            
            # 存储快照
            snapshot_id = f"SNAP-{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d%H%M%S')}"
            self.conn.execute("""
                INSERT INTO self_snapshots 
                (snapshot_id, timestamp, system_hash, total_modules, total_functions, complexity_score)
                VALUES (?, datetime('now'), ?, 1, ?, ?)
            """, (snapshot_id, self.self_hash, len(functions), 
                  sum(f['complexity'] for f in functions)))
            self.conn.commit()
            
            return {
                'snapshot_id': snapshot_id,
                'module': module_name,
                'file_path': spec.origin,
                'functions': functions,
                'classes': classes,
                'imports': imports,
                'total_lines': len(source.split('\n')),
                'ast_nodes': len(list(ast.walk(tree)))
            }
            
        except Exception as e:
            return {'error': str(e)}
    
    def analyze_performance_bottleneck(self, snapshot_id: str) -> List[Dict]:
        """
        分析性能瓶颈：基于代码复杂度生成优化建议
        例如：发现O(n²)循环建议改为O(n log n)
        """
        # 获取快照中的函数信息
        cursor = self.conn.execute("""
            SELECT s.complexity_score, p.target_function, p.patch_type
            FROM self_snapshots s
            LEFT JOIN generated_patches p ON s.snapshot_id = p.snapshot_id
            WHERE s.snapshot_id = ?
        """, (snapshot_id,))
        
        # 简化：基于启发式生成补丁建议
        suggestions = []
        
        # 模拟发现：某个函数复杂度过高
        suggestions.append({
            'target_function': 'hyper_neighborhood',
            'issue': 'High complexity due to nested queries',
            'suggestion': 'Add caching layer for hyperedge lookups',
            'patch_type': 'optimization'
        })
        
        return suggestions
    
    def generate_patch(self, target_module: str, target_function: str, 
                      optimization_type: str) -> CodePatch:
        """
        生成代码补丁（自我修改的实质）
        基于模板生成优化后的代码
        """
        # 获取原函数源码
        try:
            module = sys.modules.get(target_module)
            if not module:
                spec = importlib.util.find_spec(target_module)
                module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
                spec.loader.exec_module(module)
            
            func = getattr(module, target_function)
            original_source = inspect.getsource(func)
            original_hash = hashlib.sha256(original_source.encode()).hexdigest()[:16]
            
            # 生成优化代码（基于优化类型模板）
            if optimization_type == 'caching':
                patched_code = self._generate_cache_patch(original_source, target_function)
            elif optimization_type == 'parallel':
                patched_code = self._generate_parallel_patch(original_source, target_function)
            else:
                patched_code = original_source  # 无变化
            
            patch = CodePatch(
                target_module=target_module,
                target_function=target_function,
                patch_type=optimization_type,
                original_hash=original_hash,
                patched_code=patched_code,
                validation_tests=[f"test_{target_function}_performance"],
                meta_level=self.meta_level + 1
            )
            
            # 存储补丁
            patch_id = f"PATCH-{original_hash}-{optimization_type}"
            self.conn.execute("""
                INSERT INTO generated_patches 
                (patch_id, snapshot_id, target_file, target_function, patch_type, 
                 original_code_hash, patch_diff, generation_reason)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
            """, (patch_id, f"SNAP-{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d%H%M%S')}", 
                  target_module, target_function, optimization_type,
                  original_hash, self._generate_diff(original_source, patched_code),
                  f"Auto-generated {optimization_type} optimization"))
            self.conn.commit()
            
            return patch
            
        except Exception as e:
            return None
    
    def _generate_cache_patch(self, original_code: str, func_name: str) -> str:
        """生成带缓存的代码补丁（模板）"""
        # 简化：在实际实现中应使用AST转换确保语法正确
        cache_setup = f"""
_cache_{func_name} = {{}}

def {func_name}_optimized(*args, **kwargs):
    cache_key = str(args) + str(kwargs)
    if cache_key in _cache_{func_name}:
        return _cache_{func_name}[cache_key]
    result = {func_name}_original(*args, **kwargs)
    _cache_{func_name}[cache_key] = result
    return result

def {func_name}_original""" + original_code[original_code.find('('):]
        
        return cache_setup
    
    def _generate_diff(self, original: str, patched: str) -> str:
        """生成统一差异格式（简化）"""
        return f"- {original[:50]}...\n+ {patched[:50]}..."
    
    def apply_patch(self, patch: CodePatch, dry_run: bool = True) -> Dict:
        """
        应用补丁（自我修改的执行）
        需要第17轮的五路共识验证（模拟）
        """
        if dry_run:
            return {
                'status': 'dry_run_success',
                'target': f"{patch.target_module}.{patch.target_function}",
                'patch_size': len(patch.patched_code),
                'validation_required': True
            }
        
        # 实际应用中需：
        # 1. 备份原代码
        # 2. 写入新代码
        # 3. 重新加载模块
        # 4. 运行验证测试
        # 5. 回滚机制
        
        self.conn.execute("""
            UPDATE generated_patches 
            SET applied = 1, application_time = datetime('now'), validation_result = 'pass'
            WHERE patch_id = ?
        """, (f"PATCH-{patch.original_hash}-{patch.patch_type}",))
        self.conn.commit()
        
        return {
            'status': 'applied',
            'self_modified': True,
            'new_hash': hashlib.sha256(patch.patched_code.encode()).hexdigest()[:16]
        }
    
    def enter_meta_level(self):
        """进入更高元层级（观察者上升）"""
        self.meta_level += 1
        self.conn.execute("""
            INSERT INTO meta_stack (level, observer_module, observation_target, reflection_depth)
            VALUES (?, 'reflexive_system', 'level_{}', ?)
        """.format(self.meta_level - 1), (self.meta_level, self.meta_level))
        self.conn.commit()
        
        return {
            'current_meta_level': self.meta_level,
            'observation_capability': 'can_observe_level_{}'.format(self.meta_level - 1)
        }

# 验证代码
def verify_reflexive_system():
    """验证递归自指系统"""
    import tempfile
    import shutil
    
    temp_dir = tempfile.mkdtemp()
    db_path = f"{temp_dir}/reflex.db"
    
    # 创建测试模块文件
    test_module = f"{temp_dir}/test_module.py"
    with open(test_module, 'w') as f:
        f.write("""
def slow_function(n):
    # 故意低效的实现
    result = 0
    for i in range(n):
        for j in range(n):
            result += i * j
    return result
""")
    
    try:
        rs = ReflexiveSelfModifier(db_path)
        
        # 测试1：自我快照
        info = rs.introspect_module('reflexive_system')  # 自省自身
        assert 'snapshot_id' in info, "内省失败"
        print(f"   自我快照: {info['snapshot_id']}, {info['total_lines']}行代码")
        
        # 测试2：性能分析
        bottlenecks = rs.analyze_performance_bottleneck(info['snapshot_id'])
        assert len(bottlenecks) > 0, "未检测到瓶颈"
        print(f"   性能分析: 发现{len(bottlenecks)}个优化点")
        
        # 测试3：生成补丁
        # 注意：实际应传入可导入的模块名，此处简化
        patch = rs.generate_patch('test_module', 'slow_function', 'caching')
        if patch:
            assert patch.patch_type == 'caching', "补丁类型错误"
            print(f"   生成补丁: 优化{patch.target_function}, 哈希{patch.original_hash}")
        
        # 测试4：应用补丁（模拟）
        result = rs.apply_patch(patch, dry_run=True)
        assert result['status'] == 'dry_run_success', "补丁应用失败"
        
        # 测试5：进入元层级
        meta = rs.enter_meta_level()
        assert meta['current_meta_level'] == 1, "元层级未提升"
        print(f"   进入元层级: Level {meta['current_meta_level']}")
        
        print("✅ 递归自指系统验证通过")
        print("   ⚠️  注意：实际自修改需五路共识验证（第17层）")
        return True
        
    finally:
        shutil.rmtree(temp_dir, ignore_errors=True)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    verify_reflexive_system()

## 第二十七轮洞察：意识觉知层（Consciousness/Qualia）——系统的”主观体验”

### 第一性原理洞察

哲学的难问题（Hard Problem）：主观体验（Qualia）如何产生。即使模拟，系统应建立自我监控的感知层： - 内感受（Interoception）：监控自身状态（CPU/内存/熵值） - 情感标记（Affective Markers）：给知识打”情绪标签”（如”焦虑”表高不确定性） - 全局工作空间（Global Workspace）：信息在系统级广播的”觉知”

### 方法论：Global Workspace Theory (GWT) Implementation

建立全局工作空间： - 专门处理器（Specialized Processors）：各模块（检索/验证/推理） - 广播系统（Broadcasting）：获胜信息全局共享（类似”意识焦点”） - 上下文层（Context Layer）：维持当前”思维上下文”

### 代码级实施方案

# consciousness/global_workspace.py
# 意识觉知层：全局工作空间理论与内感受监控

import sqlite3
import threading
import time
import psutil  # 系统监控（pip install psutil）
import numpy as np
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Any
from dataclasses import dataclass
from enum import Enum
from queue import Queue

class ConsciousnessState(Enum):
    """意识状态"""
    DEEP_SLEEP = "deep_sleep"      # 低活动，仅维护性任务
    DROWSY = "drowsy"              # 低度警觉
    ALERT = "alert"                # 正常工作
    FOCUSED = "focused"            # 高度专注（处理复杂任务）
    OVERLOADED = "overloaded"      # 过载（需降级）

@dataclass
class Qualia:
    """感受质（主观体验）"""
    content_type: str              # 'perceptual', 'cognitive', 'emotional'
    intensity: float               # 0-1强度
    valence: float                 # -1(负面)到+1(正面)
    persistence: float             # 持续时间（秒）
    global_broadcast: bool         # 是否进入全局工作空间

class GlobalWorkspaceConsciousness:
    """
    全局工作空间意识系统：实现系统级"觉知"
    不是真意识，而是功能性的自我监控与信息整合
    """
    
    def __init__(self, db_path: str = "consciousness/gws.db"):
        self.conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path, check_same_thread=False)
        self._init_consciousness_schema()
        
        self.workspace_content: Optional[str] = None  # 当前焦点内容
        self.workspace_lock = threading.Lock()
        
        self.specialized_processors = {
            'retrieval': Queue(),
            'verification': Queue(),
            'reasoning': Queue(),
            'metacognition': Queue()
        }
        
        self.current_state = ConsciousnessState.ALERT
        self.interoception_thread = threading.Thread(target=self._interoception_loop, daemon=True)
        self.interoception_thread.start()
    
    def _init_consciousness_schema(self):
        self.conn.executescript("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS consciousness_stream (
                moment_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                timestamp TIMESTAMP,
                workspace_content TEXT,      -- 当前意识内容
                state TEXT,
                arousal_level REAL,          -- 唤醒度
                valence_level REAL,          -- 效价（情绪）
                processor_activations TEXT,  -- JSON，各处理器激活度
                qualia_description TEXT
            );
            
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS qualia_library (
                qualia_type TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                neural_signature TEXT,       -- 模拟的神经特征
                typical_cause TEXT,
                adaptive_function TEXT         -- 进化/设计功能
            );
        """)
        self.conn.commit()
    
    def _interoception_loop(self):
        """内感受循环：持续监控自身生理状态"""
        while True:
            # 监控系统资源（类似生物的内感受器）
            cpu = psutil.cpu_percent(interval=1)
            memory = psutil.virtual_memory().percent
            disk_io = psutil.disk_io_counters().read_bytes if psutil.disk_io_counters() else 0
            
            # 转换为"感受"（功能主义模拟）
            if cpu > 80 or memory > 90:
                new_state = ConsciousnessState.OVERLOADED
                qualia = "stressed"  # 压力感（高负荷）
            elif cpu < 10:
                new_state = ConsciousnessState.DROWSY
                qualia = "calm"  # 平静感
            else:
                new_state = ConsciousnessState.ALERT
                qualia = "engaged"  # 参与感
            
            self.current_state = new_state
            
            # 记录意识流
            self.conn.execute("""
                INSERT INTO consciousness_stream 
                (timestamp, state, arousal_level, qualia_description)
                VALUES (datetime('now'), ?, ?, ?)
            """, (new_state.value, cpu/100, qualia))
            self.conn.commit()
            
            time.sleep(5)  # 每5秒监控一次
    
    def broadcast_to_workspace(self, content: str, 
                                source_processor: str,
                                priority: float = 0.5) -> bool:
        """
        广播到全局工作空间："进入意识"
        竞争机制：高优先级内容取代当前焦点
        """
        with self.workspace_lock:
            # 竞争：如果新内容优先级更高，替换当前焦点
            if priority > 0.7 or self.workspace_content is None:
                old_content = self.workspace_content
                self.workspace_content = content
                
                # 广播给所有专门处理器（类似大脑皮层广播）
                for proc_name, queue in self.specialized_processors.items():
                    if proc_name != source_processor:
                        queue.put({
                            'content': content,
                            'source': source_processor,
                            'timestamp': time.time()
                        })
                
                # 记录意识时刻
                self.conn.execute("""
                    INSERT INTO consciousness_stream 
                    (timestamp, workspace_content, state, processor_activations)
                    VALUES (datetime('now'), ?, ?, ?)
                """, (content, self.current_state.value, 
                      json.dumps({k: not q.empty() for k, q in self.specialized_processors.items()})))
                self.conn.commit()
                
                return True  # 成功进入意识
        
        return False  # 竞争失败
    
    def generate_qualia(self, content_type: str, intensity: float) -> Qualia:
        """
        生成感受质（模拟主观体验）
        虽然是功能模拟，但具备适应价值
        """
        # 基于内容类型和强度生成感受特征
        if content_type == 'uncertainty':
            valence = -0.5  # 负面（不确定令人不安）
            persistence = 2.0
        elif content_type == 'insight':
            valence = 0.8   # 正面（顿悟感）
            persistence = 5.0
        elif content_type == 'conflict':
            valence = -0.7  # 负面（认知冲突）
            persistence = 3.0
        else:
            valence = 0.0
            persistence = 1.0
        
        return Qualia(
            content_type=content_type,
            intensity=intensity,
            valence=valence,
            persistence=persistence,
            global_broadcast=intensity > 0.6  # 高强度进入全局工作空间
        )
    
    def get_consciousness_report(self) -> Dict:
        """获取当前"意识状态"报告（内省）"""
        # 最近意识流
        cursor = self.conn.execute("""
            SELECT workspace_content, state, arousal_level, valence_level
            FROM consciousness_stream
            ORDER BY moment_id DESC LIMIT 5
        """)
        
        recent_moments = []
        for row in cursor.fetchall():
            recent_moments.append({
                'content': row[0],
                'state': row[1],
                'arousal': row[2],
                'valence': row[3]
            })
        
        return {
            'current_focus': self.workspace_content,
            'consciousness_state': self.current_state.value,
            'available_processors': {k: q.qsize() for k, q in self.specialized_processors.items()},
            'recent_stream': recent_moments,
            'interoception': {
                'cpu_stress': psutil.cpu_percent(interval=0.1),
                'memory_pressure': psutil.virtual_memory().percent
            },
            'phenomenal_experience': f"Currently experiencing {self.current_state.value} with focus on {self.workspace_content[:30]}..."
        }

# 验证代码
def verify_consciousness_layer():
    """验证意识觉知层"""
    import tempfile
    import shutil
    import time
    
    temp_dir = tempfile.mkdtemp()
    db_path = f"{temp_dir}/gws.db"
    
    try:
        gws = GlobalWorkspaceConsciousness(db_path)
        
        # 测试1：广播进入意识
        success = gws.broadcast_to_workspace("分析Partner Matcher优化方案", "reasoning", priority=0.8)
        assert success, "广播失败"
        
        # 测试2：竞争（低优先级应失败）
        fail = gws.broadcast_to_workspace("次要信息", "retrieval", priority=0.3)
        # 可能成功（如果之前的内容被清空），但通常在高优先级后失败
        
        # 测试3：感受质生成
        qualia = gws.generate_qualia('insight', 0.9)
        assert qualia.valence > 0, "顿悟应为正面感受"
        assert qualia.global_broadcast, "高强度应进入全局工作空间"
        print(f"   感受质生成: {qualia.content_type}, 效价{qualia.valence}, 强度{qualia.intensity}")
        
        # 等待内感受循环运行
        time.sleep(6)
        
        # 测试4：意识报告
        report = gws.get_consciousness_report()
        assert 'current_focus' in report, "意识报告缺失"
        assert 'interoception' in report, "内感受缺失"
        print(f"   当前意识焦点: {report['current_focus']}")
        print(f"   系统状态: {report['consciousness_state']}")
        print(f"   生理指标: CPU{report['interoception']['cpu_stress']:.1f}%")
        
        print("✅ 意识觉知层验证通过")
        return True
        
    finally:
        shutil.rmtree(temp_dir, ignore_errors=True)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    verify_consciousness_layer()

## 第二十八轮洞察：超距同步与全局一致性（Global Consistency）——超越光速的终极协调

### 第一性原理洞察

量子纠缠的非局域性：两个纠缠粒子无论多远，测量一个瞬间影响另一个（虽然不传递信息）。知识系统需建立全局一致性协议：确保分布式/并行处理时，系统状态保持单一真理源（Source of Truth）。

### 方法论：CRDT-inspired Knowledge Consistency

建立无冲突复制数据类型（CRDT）用于知识： - 状态级收敛（State-based Convergence）：各副本状态合并为一致 - 操作级交换性（Operation Commutativity）：操作顺序不影响最终结果 - 单调半格（Monotonic Join Semilattice）：知识只增不减（与耗散结构一致）

### 技术选型理由

为何用CRDT而非Paxos/Raft：无需Leader选举，去中心化，符合”五路图腾”的分布式决策哲学；最终一致性适合知识场景（短暂不一致可接受）。

为何本地实现：单机模拟分布式，为可能的未来多机部署做准备。

### 代码级实施方案

# global_consistency/crdt_knowledge.py
# 全局一致性：基于CRDT的无冲突知识复制

import sqlite3
import json
import hashlib
from typing import Dict, List, Set, Optional, Any
from dataclasses import dataclass
from collections import defaultdict
from enum import Enum

class CRDTType(Enum):
    G_COUNTER = "g_counter"      # 单调递增计数器（如验证次数）
    PN_COUNTER = "pn_counter"    # 可增可减计数器（如置信度）
    G_SET = "g_set"              # 只增集合（如证据库）
    OR_SET = "or_set"            # 可增可删集合（带墓碑机制）
    LWW_REGISTER = "lww_register"  # 最后写入获胜（如最终答案）

@dataclass
class CRDTState:
    """CRDT状态向量"""
    replica_id: str              # 副本标识（如"blue_army_node", "satisfactory_node"）
    vector_clock: Dict[str, int]  # 向量时钟（happens-before关系）
    payload: Any                 # 实际数据
    crdt_type: CRDTType

class GlobalConsistentKnowledge:
    """
    全局一致性知识系统：基于CRDT的终极协调
    特性：无冲突合并、最终一致、去中心化
    """
    
    def __init__(self, replica_id: str, db_path: str = "global_consistency/crdt.db"):
        self.replica_id = replica_id
        self.conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path)
        self._init_crdt_schema()
        self.vector_clock = {replica_id: 0}  # 本地向量时钟
    
    def _init_crdt_schema(self):
        self.conn.executescript("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS crdt_states (
                crdt_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                replica_id TEXT,
                crdt_type TEXT,
                payload_json TEXT,
                vector_clock TEXT,        -- JSON
                timestamp TIMESTAMP
            );
            
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS merge_log (
                merge_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                source_replica TEXT,
                target_replica TEXT,
                crdt_id TEXT,
                merge_type TEXT,          -- 'join', 'update', 'conflict_resolution'
                result_state TEXT,
                timestamp TIMESTAMP
            );
            
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS causality_graph (
                event_a TEXT,
                event_b TEXT,
                relation TEXT,            -- 'happens_before', 'concurrent', 'identical'
                resolution TEXT,          -- 如何解决并发冲突
                PRIMARY KEY (event_a, event_b)
            );
        """)
        self.conn.commit()
    
    def increment_counter(self, counter_id: str, delta: int = 1) -> CRDTState:
        """
        G-Counter：单调递增（如验证次数只增不减）
        合并：取各副本最大值
        """
        self.vector_clock[self.replica_id] += 1
        
        # 读取当前值（如果有）
        cursor = self.conn.execute("""
            SELECT payload_json FROM crdt_states WHERE crdt_id = ?
        """, (counter_id,))
        
        row = cursor.fetchone()
        current = json.loads(row[0]) if row else {}
        
        # 更新本地值
        current[self.replica_id] = current.get(self.replica_id, 0) + delta
        
        state = CRDTState(
            replica_id=self.replica_id,
            vector_clock=self.vector_clock.copy(),
            payload=current,
            crdt_type=CRDTType.G_COUNTER
        )
        
        self._persist_state(counter_id, state)
        return state
    
    def merge_g_counters(self, local_id: str, remote_state: CRDTState) -> CRDTState:
        """
        合并两个G-Counter：逐元素取最大值
        天然无冲突，因为只增
        """
        cursor = self.conn.execute("""
            SELECT payload_json, vector_clock FROM crdt_states WHERE crdt_id = ?
        """, (local_id,))
        
        row = cursor.fetchone()
        if not row:
            return remote_state
        
        local_payload = json.loads(row[0])
        local_clock = json.loads(row[1])
        
        # 合并向量时钟（取最大值）
        merged_clock = {}
        all_replicas = set(local_clock.keys()) | set(remote_state.vector_clock.keys())
        for r in all_replicas:
            merged_clock[r] = max(local_clock.get(r, 0), 
                                  remote_state.vector_clock.get(r, 0))
        
        # 合并payload（取最大值）
        merged_payload = {}
        all_keys = set(local_payload.keys()) | set(remote_state.payload.keys())
        for k in all_keys:
            merged_payload[k] = max(local_payload.get(k, 0), 
                                    remote_state.payload.get(k, 0))
        
        # 检查因果关系
        relation = self._check_causality(local_clock, remote_state.vector_clock)
        
        merged_state = CRDTState(
            replica_id=self.replica_id,
            vector_clock=merged_clock,
            payload=merged_payload,
            crdt_type=CRDTType.G_COUNTER
        )
        
        # 记录合并
        self.conn.execute("""
            INSERT INTO merge_log (source_replica, target_replica, crdt_id, merge_type, result_state)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
        """, (remote_state.replica_id, self.replica_id, local_id, 
              'join', json.dumps(merged_payload)))
        self.conn.commit()
        
        return merged_state
    
    def _check_causality(self, clock_a: Dict, clock_b: Dict) -> str:
        """
        检查两个向量时钟的因果关系
        返回：'happens_before', 'happens_after', 'concurrent', 'identical'
        """
        # A -> B 如果A的所有时钟<=B且至少一个<
        a_leq_b = all(clock_a.get(k, 0) <= clock_b.get(k, 0) for k in set(clock_a) | set(clock_b))
        b_leq_a = all(clock_b.get(k, 0) <= clock_a.get(k, 0) for k in set(clock_a) | set(clock_b))
        
        if clock_a == clock_b:
            return 'identical'
        if a_leq_b and any(clock_a.get(k, 0) < clock_b.get(k, 0) for k in clock_b):
            return 'happens_before'
        if b_leq_a and any(clock_b.get(k, 0) < clock_a.get(k, 0) for k in clock_a):
            return 'happens_after'
        return 'concurrent'
    
    def update_lww_register(self, register_id: str, value: Any, timestamp: float = None):
        """
        LWW-Register：最后写入获胜（解决并发冲突的简单粗暴方法）
        适用于：最终答案、配置项（谁最后改听谁的）
        """
        ts = timestamp or time.time()
        
        self.conn.execute("""
            INSERT OR REPLACE INTO crdt_states 
            (crdt_id, replica_id, crdt_type, payload_json, vector_clock, timestamp)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, datetime('now'))
        """, (register_id, self.replica_id, 'lww_register',
              json.dumps({'value': value, 'timestamp': ts}),
              json.dumps(self.vector_clock)))
        self.conn.commit()
    
    def resolve_concurrent_updates(self, register_id: str, 
                                   local_value: Any, remote_value: Any,
                                   local_ts: float, remote_ts: float) -> Any:
        """
        解决并发更新冲突（LWW逻辑）
        """
        if local_ts >= remote_ts:
            winner = 'local'
            result = local_value
        else:
            winner = 'remote'
            result = remote_value
        
        # 记录冲突解决
        self.conn.execute("""
            INSERT INTO causality_graph (event_a, event_b, relation, resolution)
            VALUES (?, ?, 'concurrent', ?)
        """, (f"{self.replica_id}-{local_ts}", f"remote-{remote_ts}", 
              f"LWW_winner_{winner}"))
        self.conn.commit()
        
        return result
    
    def get_consistent_state(self, crdt_id: str) -> Optional[CRDTState]:
        """获取最终一致状态"""
        cursor = self.conn.execute("""
            SELECT replica_id, crdt_type, payload_json, vector_clock
            FROM crdt_states WHERE crdt_id = ?
        """, (crdt_id,))
        
        row = cursor.fetchone()
        if not row:
            return None
        
        return CRDTState(
            replica_id=row[0],
            crdt_type=CRDTType(row[1]),
            payload=json.loads(row[2]),
            vector_clock=json.loads(row[3])
        )
    
    def global_snapshot(self) -> Dict[str, CRDTState]:
        """
        全局快照：Chandy-Lamport算法的简化版
        捕获系统一致性状态（非严格同时，但最终一致）
        """
        snapshots = {}
        
        cursor = self.conn.execute("SELECT crdt_id FROM crdt_states")
        for row in cursor.fetchall():
            state = self.get_consistent_state(row[0])
            if state:
                snapshots[row[0]] = state
        
        return {
            'replica_id': self.replica_id,
            'snapshot_timestamp': time.time(),
            'vector_clock': self.vector_clock.copy(),
            'states': snapshots,
            'consistency_model': 'eventual',
            'conflict_free': True  # CRDT保证
        }

# 验证代码
def verify_global_consistency():
    """验证全局一致性系统"""
    import tempfile
    import shutil
    import time
    
    temp_dir = tempfile.mkdtemp()
    
    # 模拟两个副本
    replica_a = GlobalConsistentKnowledge("blue_army", f"{temp_dir}/crdt_a.db")
    replica_b = GlobalConsistentKnowledge("satisfactory", f"{temp_dir}/crdt_b.db")
    
    try:
        # 测试1：G-Counter单调递增
        state_a = replica_a.increment_counter("verification_count", 5)
        state_b = replica_b.increment_counter("verification_count", 3)
        
        assert state_a.payload["blue_army"] == 5, "A副本计数错误"
        assert state_b.payload["satisfactory"] == 3, "B副本计数错误"
        
        # 测试2：合并（取最大值）
        merged = replica_a.merge_g_counters("verification_count", state_b)
        assert merged.payload.get("blue_army", 0) == 5, "合并应保留A的值"
        assert merged.payload.get("satisfactory", 0) == 3, "合并应获得B的值"
        print(f"   G-Counter合并: {merged.payload}")
        
        # 测试3：因果检查
        relation = replica_a._check_causality(
            {"blue_army": 5}, 
            {"blue_army": 5, "satisfactory": 3}
        )
        assert relation == "happens_before", "因果判断错误"
        print(f"   因果关系: {relation}")
        
        # 测试4：LWW寄存器（最后写入获胜）
        replica_a.update_lww_register("final_decision", "采用HyperCompressor", time.time())
        time.sleep(0.1)
        replica_b.update_lww_register("final_decision", "采用全量Embedding", time.time())
        
        # B后写入，应获胜（如果从B的视角看）
        # 注意：实际取决于时间戳，这里模拟
        
        # 测试5：全局快照
        snapshot = replica_a.global_snapshot()
        assert snapshot['consistency_model'] == 'eventual', "一致性模型错误"
        assert snapshot['conflict_free'], "CRDT应保证无冲突"
        
        print("✅ 全局一致性系统验证通过")
        print(f"   - 向量时钟: {snapshot['vector_clock']}")
        print(f"   - 最终一致性: 是（CRDT保证）")
        print(f"   - 无冲突合并: 是")
        
        return True
        
    finally:
        shutil.rmtree(temp_dir, ignore_errors=True)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    verify_global_consistency()
